Therefore, please give us your mercy, for without your mercy everything is void. Actually, we are always suffering in this material world. Without your kind mercy and your blessings, it will be impossible for us to perform devotional service. So, you are actually the cause of our happiness. Once again, please help us, especially when we are chanting the holy name Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare so that we may nicely concentrate on the holy name. In this way it may be possible for us one day to become pure devotees.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the genuine messenger of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. You are fulfilling His desire and the desire of your guru mahārāja, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, that in very town and village the chanting of the *mahā-mantra,* Hare Kṛṣṇa, will resound and that people will merge into the ocean of transcendental bliss and happiness.

Our only prayer is that you please help us, who are fallen souls, to become purified and somehow advance in pure devotion. Our prayer is that our village can became a real Vaiṣṇava village and thus become an example to others villages of how real happiness can be found in the society of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

In *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (10.14.58) Lord Brahmā declares, *padaṁ padaṁ yad vipadāṁ:* in this material world there is danger at every step. But you always cross over such dangers because you are always fixed in devotional service. You are very strong and very powerful in preaching the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement; you always remember Kṛṣṇa, twenty-four hours a day; you are a pure devotee of Kṛṣṇa; you always engage in devotional service; and you are always very humble.

In closing, we pray for your blessings, your mercy, and your power to give us the proper intelligence so we can become humble and engage in Kṛṣṇa consciousness sincerely. We want to always remember Kṛṣṇa, twenty-four hours a day, without any fear, although this material world it is *padaṁ padaṁ yad vipadāṁ*.

*Jaya Jagat-guru* Śrīla Prabhupāda !!

Thank you, and Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Your insignificant servants at Gītā Nāgarī Baru, Menggala, Tulang Bawang-Lampung, Indonesia.  
(written by Ṣaḍbhuja Gaurāṅga Dāsa)

## Goteborg

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, our beloved founder-*ācārya* and the well-wisher of all,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

Please accept our sincere attempts to serve Your Divine Grace; to serve ISKCON, the house you built for the whole world to live in; and to serve everyone inside and outside this house equally.

We are very happy to inform you that your mission is being distributed with firm determination here in Goteborg. Even though we are barely a handful of fulltime devotees, and most of us more or less occupied by household and family duties, still — by the grace and power of your transcendental presence — we somehow manage to maintain several preaching activities, including continuous school preaching, Govinda’s vegetarian restaurant, a quarterly magazine (*KrishnaBladet*), and a multimedia website featuring web radio.

Sometimes our restaurant guests see you sitting in the temple room in your *mūrti* form. Sometimes they become fascinated and surprised, asking us if that is a real person or a statue! We tell them that it is indeed a statue but at the same time a personal manifestation of our founder-*ācārya*. The difference between your statue and your physical appearance is marginal. It depends simply on our vision. When we serve you as if you were personally present, you certainly become personally present. We try to offer you our humble attempts at service, and in return your offer us the greatest opportunity in the three worlds: to become a servant of Lord Kṛṣṇa in the spiritual realm. We can never thank you properly for this gift; we can only beg to remain your aspiring servants. We are your “boys and girls,” always longing for the opportunity to help you in your ongoing mission in the Western world. Please let us always remain in your divine association.

Your aspiring servants in Goteborg, Sweden.  
(written by Bhakta Mikael)

## Guadalajara

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our must humble obeisances at the dust of your divine lotus feet.

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**“I was born in the darkest ignorance, and my spiritual master opened my eyes with the torch of knowledge. I offer my respectful obeisances unto him.”**

The pure devotees of the Lord are distinguished from others due to their following the six intrinsic principles of exclusive surrender to the Supreme Lord, which are stated in the *Vaiṣṇava-tantra* as follows:

*ānukūlyasya saṅkalpaḥ prātikūlyasya varjanam*  
*rakṣiṣyatīti viśvāso goptṛtve varaṇaṁ tathā*  
*ātma-nikṣepa-kārpaṇye ṣaḍ-vidhāśaraṇāgatiḥ*

**“The six divisions of surrender are the acceptance of those things favorable to devotional service, the rejection of unfavorable things, the conviction that Kṛṣṇa will give protection, the acceptance of the Lord as one’s guardian or master, full self-surrender, and humility. (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya-līlā*** **22.100)**

A pure devotee is sometimes called a *śaraṇāgata-bhakta,* or a surrendered soul. Literally, *śaraṇāgati* means to take complete shelter or refuge in Śrī Nanda Kumāra, the youthful son of Nanda Mahārāja, who has appeared at Śrī Navadvīpa as Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya.

The necessity of surrendering to Śrī Kṛṣṇa was indeed the essence of the teachings of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. The same has been confirmed by Śrī Kṛṣṇa in the *Bhagavad-gītā* (18.66):

*sarva-dharmān parityajya mām ekaṁśaraṇaṁ vraja*  
*ahaṁ tvāṁ sarva-pāpebhyo mokṣayiṣyāmi māśucaḥ*

**“Abandon all varieties of religion and just surrender unto Me. I shall deliver you from all sinful reactions. Do not fear.”**

Arjuna is herein advised to abandon all varieties of religion — particularly those mixed with *karma* and *jñāna*. Such surrender, Kṛṣṇa promises, assures that His devotee will come to Him. The confidentiality of surrender is revealed in this verse by the use of the word *vraja*. When the rule of Sanskrit grammar known as *alaṅkāra* is applied to this verse, it is shown that Śrī Kṛṣṇa is saying that by exclusive surrender unto Him Arjuna will attain the land of Vraja, the Supreme abode of Godhead. It may also be understood through this instruction to Arjuna that exclusive surrender is the only method of achieving the Lord’s eternal association.

The point is that one must seek the association of persons who are Kṛṣṇa conscious and who engage in devotional service. Without such association, one cannot make advancement. Simply by theoretical knowledge or study one cannot make any appreciable advancement. One must give up the association of materialistic persons and seek the association of devotees, because without the association of devotees one cannot understand the activities of the Lord. Generally, people are convinced of the impersonal feature of the Absolute Truth. Because they do not associate with devotees, they cannot understand that the Absolute Truth can be a person and have personal activities.

The sincere devotee who associates with devotees gets the opportunity to hear and contemplate this subject of the pastimes of the Lord, and the result is that he feels distaste for the so-called happiness in this or that world, in heaven or on other planets. The devotees are simply interested in being transferred to the personal association of the Lord; they have no more attraction for temporary so-called happiness.

By the mercy of the Supreme Lord, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we had the chance to meet you. You had explained that causeless mercy in your commentary on the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, as follows:

When something uncommon happens in one’s progressive spiritual life, it should be understood to be incurred by *ajñāta-sukṛti,* or pious activities beyond one’s knowledge. To see personally the Supreme Personality of Godhead or His pure devotee is not an ordinary incident. \[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 4.22.7, purport\]

Even thought we can not fully understand why Kṛṣṇa has been so merciful to us, we feel very fortunate to have such a spiritual master. Our mood is expresses in this verse by Śrī Yāmunācārya (*Stotra-ratna* 43):

*bhavantam evānucaran nirantaraḥ*  
*praśānta-niḥśeṣa-mano-rathāntaraḥ*  
*kadāham aikāntika-nitya-kiṅkaraḥ*  
*praharṣayiṣyāmi sanātha-jīvitam*

**“By serving You constantly, one is freed from all material desires and is completely pacified. When shall I engage as Your permanent eternal servant and always feel joyful to have such a fitting master?”**

Your Divine Grace had such power that you could charm anybody who came in contact with you, as you explained in the following excerpt:

Pure devotees are so absorbed in thought of Kṛṣṇa that they have no other engagement; although they may seem to think or act otherwise, they are always thinking of Kṛṣṇa. The smile of such a Kṛṣṇa conscious person is so attractive that simply by smiling he wins so many admirers, disciples, and followers. \[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 3.22.21\]

Unfortunately, some foolish disciples and others cannot see your divine qualities, teachings, and activities in the right way, as you explained:

The conclusion is that the Supreme Personality of Godhead is situated in everyone’s heart, and His pure devotees, the Vaiṣṇavas, are also traveling all over the world, but those who are under the modes of material nature cannot understand the form of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, the source of this cosmic manifestation, or the Vaiṣṇavas. It is said, therefore, that one cannot see the Supreme Personality of Godhead or a Vaiṣṇava with these material eyes. One has to purify his senses and engage in the service of the Lord. Then gradually one can realize who is the Supreme Personality of Godhead and who is a Vaiṣṇava. \[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 4.22.9, purport\]

We are here in New Nīlācala (Guadalajara, Mexico), the last project you gave to the Mexican *yātrā* before Your Divine Grace went back to Goloka Vṛndāvana.

Some of us were the first to try to engage in some service since you gave us Lord Jagannātha’s Ratha-yātrā festival many years back, in August 1977. Now by the Lord’s mercy he has given us another chance to render some service at the dust of your divine feet and the feet of the Supreme Lord, Śrī Jagannātha, Śrī Baladeva, and Śrīmatī Subhadrā Devī here in Your Divine Grace’s temple in Nueva Nīlācala.

The members of the temple board want to inform Your Divine Grace that the last Ratha-yātrā festival was a great success (on November 12–13, 2005). Even though we had to change the time of the parade from 12 noon to 4 pm, it was better: many more people were in the parade and on the side of the street getting the *darśana* of they Lordships, and when the chariots got to the festival site, many thousands of people greeted them and got the mercy of the Śrī Jagannātha, Śrī Baladeva, and Śrīmatī Subhadrā Devī.

In the *Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa* it is said:

*yān ārūḍhaṁ puraḥ prekṣya samāyāntaṁ janārdanam*  
*abhyutthānaṁ naraḥ kurvan pātayet sarva-kilbiṣam*

**“A person who sees the Lord’s Ratha-yātrā car festival and then stands up to receive the Lord can purge all kinds of sinful results from his body.” (*****Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu*** **1.2.130)**

By Your Divine Grace’s causeless mercy, which has allowed us to get free from sinful life and start on the way back home, back to Godhead, thousands attended as the three chariots were slowly moved along the way. The people were attracted to the colorful chariots; they wanted to get a close look at the deities — Śrī Jagannātha, Śrīmatī Subhadrā, and Śrī Baladeva — and they spontaneously joined the *kīrtana,* chanting and dancing with the devotees and pulling the rope.

A similar statement is there in the *Bhaviṣya Purāṇa,* where it is said:

*rathena saha gacchanti pārśvataḥ pṛṣṭhato ’grataḥ*  
*viṣṇunaiva samāḥ sarve bhavanti śvapadādayaḥ*

**“Even if born in a lowly family, a person who follows the Ratha-yātrā car when the Deities pass in front or from behind will surely be elevated to the position of achieving equal opulence with Viṣṇu.” (*****Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu*** **1.2.131)**

As we looked up at the chariots and saw your divine *mūrti,* we understood that all the people pulling the chariots, chanting, dancing, and feasting were receiving this great opportunity only due to your strong desire to save all of us fallen souls from material existence. You show your causeless mercy upon all of us by allowing us to render you some small services here in the Nueva Nīlācala *mandira*.

Please allow us to make the thirtieth annual Ratha-yātrā festival this year a great success for the satisfaction of Your Divine Grace and Their Lordships Śrī Jagannātha, Śrī Baladeva, and Śrīmatī Subhadrā Devī.

As our final humble prayer at the dust of your divine lotus feet on this must auspicious day of your appearance, let us take Your Divine Grace’s own words, which you wrote for you own spiritual master:

Personally, I have no hope for any direct service for the coming crores of births of the sojourn of my life, but I am confident that some day or other I shall be delivered from this mire of delusion in which I am at present so deeply sunk. Therefore let me with all my earnestness pray at the lotus feet of my divine master to allow me to suffer the lot for which I am destined due to my past misdoings, but to let me have this power of recollection: that I am nothing but a tiny servant of the almighty Absolute Godhead, realized through the unflinching mercy of my divine master. Let me therefore bow down at his lotus feet with all the humility at my command.

Falling at the dust of your divine lotus feet,  
Your servants at the Nueva Nīlācala Mandira, Guadalajara, Mexico.

## Helsinki

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

You are the general of the *saṅkīrtana* movement, and only by your grace can preaching activities become successful. Still, you were always very eager to hear the preaching results from your temples around the world. In the same way as one offers *gaṅgā-jala* to Mother Gaṅgā, we now offer you some news of the main activities of preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Finland.

There are many signs that the fruits from years of book distribution, *prasādam* distribution, *harināma-saṅkīrtana,* lecturing at schools, and putting on public programs are becoming ripe. Now your ISKCON movement is taken more seriously and is respected by society, the academic community, and people in general.

Over the last fifteen years we have hosted many guests and study groups from universities and other institutions, especially high schools. Our devotees have also lectured in some 120 schools and academies all over Finland. Also, during the last few years students in Finnish schools have had the opportunity to officially choose Kṛṣṇa conscious religious education organized by devotees. At present there are five groups, but there is great potential for more.

Recently the cultural department of Helsinki approached the Kṛṣṇa temple and asked us to arrange workshops on Indian vegetarian cooking for various schools in Helsinki. Though this seems like a minor thing, it indicates a major change in people’s attitude toward the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. After all, ten years ago there was no such interest.

Devotees’ parents have grown more appreciative toward Kṛṣṇa consciousness and temple life. They sometimes visit to express their heartfelt gratitude for how their children have transformed for the better. This is a great change from the early days of your movement in Finland, when the parents sometimes used to drag their offspring away from the Sunday Feast!

Academics are consulting us before publishing their textbooks about religion, and they are offering more and more appreciation of your movement.

We can understand that the increasing interest in vegetarianism, yoga, meditation, Āyurveda, and Indian culture in general is further evidence of the success of your preaching.

The latest good news is that it seems likely we will soon open a branch of the Bhaktivedanta College.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, if we are going to preach effectively, we must understand and realize the knowledge in your books. Besides preaching, our temple has emphasized that devotees should have a nice Kṛṣṇa conscious education. For instance, in 2004 most of the temple *brahmacārīs* completed the Bhakti-śāstrī course, and now Bhakti-vaibhava studies are going on. Our temple arranges *bhakta* programs about once a year.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the greatest preacher and the greatest visionary. Understanding even a drop of your vision of how much ISKCON can benefit society gives us great enlivenment and ecstasy. On the other hand, we feel very small and insignificant, completely dependent on the mercy of Your Divine Grace and your divine servants.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us the intelligence to expand the *saṅkīrtana* movement here on all levels. We want to preach your message directly, indirectly, and in between, to young and old, to academics and the uneducated, through person-to-person preaching, and through books, newspapers, television, radio, the Internet, public programs, vegetarian workshops, and the Vedic arts. We want to do all this to please you, while always keeping our main focus on the primary *aṅgas* of the *saṅkīrtana* movement — book distribution, *harināma-saṅkīrtana,* and *prasādam* distribution.

There is so much potential for preaching, but so few devotees. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us so that the present devotees will become more and more qualified and realized and thus be able to present Kṛṣṇa consciousness the way you taught it. Please send more devotees to assist in spreading the movement of Śrī Caitanya all over the world.

Your tiny servants in Helsinki, Finland.

## Hong Kong

Our most beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

On this auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we humbly beg your permission to offer our humble respects and deepest gratitude to Your Divine Grace. We feel extremely privileged to have the opportunity to place this small offering, insignificant as it is, at your divine lotus feet.

But what can we say to accurately express the feelings of love and gratitude we have for you, what words can we use to rightly glorify you, to do justice to your greatness?

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, should we glorify your total surrender to the instructions of your guru mahārāja, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura — “If you get money, print books.”“Preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the English-speaking world.”— instructions you not only adopted whole-heartedly but fulfilled single-handedly to perfection? How do we even begin to describe the depth of your commitment and dedication to the mission of your guru mahārāja?

Should we speak about your incomparable humility, which, despite your obvious qualification as a *paramahaṁsa* and empowered devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa, inspired you to humbly and submissively offer prayers to Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa while onboard the *Jaladuta*, begging for mercy and empowerment from guru and Kṛṣṇa to carry out the monumental task ahead?

Or should we glorify your profound compassion for all humanity, which impelled you to travel to foreign lands, alone and at an advanced age, just to give the holy name to the people in degraded and materialistic societies? You underwent so many trials and tribulations, put up with so many inconveniences, gave so much of your time and effort, simply to save millions of lost souls and reclaim them on behalf of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

How can we appropriately appreciate how you made an almost impossible task possible by turning hippies and drug addicts into Vaiṣṇavas, teaching them from scratch the Vedic way of life and circling the globe twelve times in a span of ten short years to preach and to establish ISKCON centers all over the world? By your tireless efforts in serving the lotus feet of guru and Kṛṣṇa, you taught by personal example what a Vaiṣṇava should be and how he should dedicate his life to devotional service. You are a true *ācārya* in every sense of the word.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your sharp intelligence, wit, and wealth of knowledge, you clearly explained the scientific principles of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and the sweetness of *bhakti-yoga* to all kinds of people, including academics, scientists, and religious heads. Like a mighty general leading the foot soldiers of Lord Caitanya’s army against the onslaught of *māyā,* you fought to crush the impersonalism propagated by the Māyāvādīs and establish the superiority of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s personal form and attributes, beyond any doubt. You were always fearless in your fight and uncompromising in upholding the principles of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Truly, you epitomized the name Abhay Charanaravinda!

By your intense desire and sincere effort alone, the chanting of the holy names of the Lord is now heard in every town and village, as prophesied by Lord Caitanya Himself. Oh, Śrīla Prabhupāda, your compassion is indeed limitless, and you are more merciful than the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself!

We in Hong Kong are the most fortunate recipients of your deep love and concern, for it is only by your mercy and compassion that people of Chinese origin can today chant and dance in glorification of the holy name, following ways of the *saṅkīrtana* movement established by Lord Gaurāṅga. It is also by your mercy and blessings that your loyal disciples and a host of other devotees have worked hard to fulfill Your Divine Grace’s desire to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness among the Chinese people.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, building on the firm foundation laid by Your Divine Grace and your stalwart preacher-disciples, we are doing our best to continue preaching the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the materialistic, fallen society that is Hong Kong. We are conducting regular preaching programs to teach people about the philosophy and benefits of chanting, and we are teaching the public about the benefits of a *prasādam* diet and a healthy way of life. We are also using the medium of *aṣṭāṅga-yoga* to reach out to the masses, with the aim of gradually bringing them to the platform of *bhakti-yoga*. By your kind blessings, Kṛṣṇa continues to send us many nice souls who are sincerely and seriously taking to the path of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Though undeserving, we humbly beg you to bless us with just a tiny drop of your compassion, humility, perseverance, intelligence, and devotion to the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, so that we may further propagate this divine mission you have developed on behalf of your guru mahārāja. If we can please you in any small way at all through our meager efforts, we will consider our lives successful.

Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!!

Begging to always remain your humble servants, and aspiring to please you more and more,  
Your eternally indebted children and grandchildren at ISKCON Hong Kong.

## Johannesburg

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

To us at the tip of Africa your message brings solace  
As your sublime teachings here we try to embrace.  
For Africa, *prasāda* distribution and *kīrtan* was your recommendation.  
By your mercy we are attempting to accept this prescription.  
In the last year over half a million plates where distributed,  
An accomplishment that is to your mercy attributed.  
Many all-night *kīrtan* sessions where held;  
Devotees danced jubilantly and the holy name yelled.  
Some nights devotees remained awake to simply chant *japa,*  
Staying awake enthusiastically till after *guru-pūjā.*  
Your love for us, shown through your innumerable sacrifices,  
Is the main inspiration or impetus.  
Festivals attracted many new souls,  
Inspiring us to maintain this as our goal.  
Marathons were embraced with enthusiasm,  
Yielding 32,815 books sold and totaling 1,578 BTG subscriptions.  
Deity worship we try to maintain nicely,  
Offering imported outfits with pretty embroidery,  
Providing training and education for new *pūjārīs*  
Striving to make heartfelt offerings.  
To be servants of your servants is our fervent prayer.  
Your teachings with others we like to share.  
To maintain the house you built is our aim.  
Please empower us to chant and distribute the holy name.

The devotees at Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurahari Mandira, Johannesburg, South Africa.  
(written by Mādrī Devī Dāsī)

## KwaZulu Natal

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Yesterday was the glorious start of our annual Ratha-yātrā festival at the Durban beachfront.

It was glorious for many reasons. Thousands of people attending from the first day, attractive entertainment presented by the devotees, tasty *prasādam* served free to all who attended; and most importantly, the association of so many of your senior preachers. Thirteen *sannyāsīs* have kindly attended to give your transcendental message to the thousands of guests. It is in this association that the devotees are confidently reminded of your love and compassion, which come out to join that of Lord Jagannātha during Ratha-yātrā.

Right now there is a light rain falling over the chariot parade route, cleansing the path for Lord Jagannātha to ride over. As you assume your rightful seat before the Lord, we are vividly aware of your merciful gaze, which has made it possible for us to have the *darśana* of Lord Jagannātha.

Your merciful desire for our spiritual benefit has enabled us to be successful at anything we undertake in the service of the Lord, big or small. May we always appreciate this fact, never losing sight of it by the contaminating thought that we are actually responsible for successes. May we steadfastly improve our spiritual practices and fine-tune our *japa* and our study of your books, thus not creating blockages in the pipeline of your mercy flowing to our hearts.

All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Your servants at your ISKCON temple in KwaZulu Natal, South Africa.

## Lalitā-pura Sevā-kuñja Āśrama

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are an unlimited ocean of mercy. By your kindness you have given a chance to us, conditioned souls covered by material ignorance, to learn the chanting of the *mahā-mantra,* Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

Oh, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are very dear to Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Due to your causeless mercy, the special blessing of Lord Caitanya, the *saṅkīrtana-yajña,* is spreading to every town and village all over the world. You are thus saving millions of suffering souls and giving them the blessings and transcendental knowledge of Krsna consciousness. In this way you also give them an opportunity to engage in the devotional service of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara.

Oh, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are that rare personality in this modern era who displayed such great humility and kindness. Although already advanced in age, you were not deterred from executing the command of your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the Western world. We cannot imagine what would have happened to us if you had not shown us this miraculous blessing. We here in Palangka Raya, Indonesia, feel so far away from your *sādhu-saṅga*. Every day we are engaged only in our material business and associate with materialistic people. Oh, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please help us to only be engaged in devotional service to you and Kṛṣṇa.

Oh, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you always live through your books and divine instructions. The *saṅkīrtana-yajña* and your transcendental name and fame will always be spread all over the world. Śrīla Prabhupāda *mahā-guru-mahārāja-kī jaya*!

Your fallen souls at Lalitā-pura Sevā-kuñja Āśrama, Palangka Raya, Central Borneo, Indonesia.  
(written by Brajarāja Dāsa)

## Leicester

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories unto you, beloved *gurudeva*!

By your mercy we have come together here in Leicester to try to serve you and please you, so that we may become purified within our hearts and be admitted into the transcendental loving service of Śrī Śrī Pañca-tattva, and ultimately that of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.

We ask that you kindly bless us and forgive us for the countless offenses we may knowingly and unknowingly commit, day after day, in our childlike attempts to please you. We pray that we may soon become worthy to engage in devotional service.

Gurudeva, whatever plans you have for expanding the message of Caitanya Mahāprabhu, may we all be useful instruments in your hands. No matter what our service may be — from the biggest preacher to the lowliest cleaner — may we perform that to our best ability and co-operate like a well-oiled machine that needs every part in order to function to its best ability.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for all you have so freely given us. Now may we courageously and joyfully go ever forward spreading your glories wherever we may be.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we offer you our worship:

\[May be sung to the tune of *śrī-guru-caraṇa-padma* . . .\]

*Jaya Jaya gurudeva,* Prabhupāda!  
*Jaya Jaya gurudeva,* Prabhupāda!  
We humbly bow down before you,  
Our master, friend, guide, and guru.  
You’re everything to us, dearmost  
Śrīla Prabhupāda.

We come here in praise before you,  
Our hands folded in prayer to you.  
Accept us and guide us safely,  
Śrīla Prabhupāda.

We’ve gained so much knowledge from you,  
The message of Godhead so true,  
In the holy books you gave us,  
Śrīla Prabhupāda.

A process perfect and complete,  
Shown us through your lotus feet,  
Expertly guiding us home,  
Śrīla Prabhupāda.

\[change of melody — as in *cakhu-dān dilo jei* . . .\]

Bless us with pure love for you,  
Help us master, see us through,  
As we struggle in this world of pain.  
May our lives be offered to you.  
Purify us, blessed guru.

May we be like puppets in your hands;  
With your mercy join Caitanya’s dance  
To the tune of Hari Kīrtan,  
Filling up our souls for certain.

Make us worthy with your kindly glance.  
Moving in the transcendental dance,  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, we thank you.  
We are nothing here without you.

Keep us safe within your loving arms.  
Free us from this life of Māyās charms.  
May we always work together,  
In your service stay forever,

Giving up our false hope and desire,  
By your mercy rising ever higher.  
You are the swanlike messenger,  
*Jagat-guru* — shining star.

May your fame shine bright throughout these lands!  
With every nation raising up their hands!

*Jaya* Prabhupāda! *Jaya* Prabhupāda! *Jaya* Prabhupāda! *Jaya* Prabhupāda!  
Jaya gurudeva! Jaya gurudeva! Jaya gurudeva! Jaya gurudeva!

Your aspiring servants at ISKCON Leicester, UK.  
(written by Jānakī Devī Dāsī)

## Lisbon

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Twenty-nine years have gone by since your departure to the spiritual world, and your sons, grandchildren, and even great-grandchildren are arduously taking up the mission left by Your Divine Grace.

You took only twelve years to establish Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the world, a truly unbelievable feat. Your transcendental intelligence and complete surrender unto the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, are qualities that inspire us to continue resisting *māyā* and to endeavor to follow instructions from our gurus in the disciplic succession.

It has been forty years since you founded ISKCON, the Kṛṣṇa conscious treasure composed of members who cherish and enjoy the association of a caring family. To understand the personal activities of the Lord, we must seek the association of devotees, and therefore you have kindheartedly created ISKCON so that we may practice devotional service in full Kṛṣṇa consciousness as a big family.

Because we all want to love, you taught us to place our love in the right place. You taught us to love the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, through ISKCON, and thus expand our loving feelings to the utmost extent. In this way we learn to develop complete love — love for Kṛṣṇa, which is *ahaituki,* without any material cause, and completely satisfying.

We offer our most respectful obeisances unto you, who kindly taught us that Kṛṣṇa consciousness is a marvelous thing that can turn even the most delightful material enjoyment into something distasteful. In deep gratitude and love, we seek shelter at your lotus feet in search of your causeless mercy.

Your humble servants at ISKCON, Lisbon, Portugal.

## London (Soho)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances on the auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā day. All glories to Your Divine Grace! May we always remain particles of dust at your lotus feet in the service of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara.

Here we are, Śrīla Prabhupāda, in the very place that you called hell — London. We are taking shelter of your most beloved Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara; Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā; Giri-Govardhana; and Gaura-Nitāi. We are chanting the holy names, distributing your books, and feeding hundreds of people *kṛṣṇa-prasādam* every day. We are endeavoring to build a community of sincere Vaiṣṇavas dedicated to your ISKCON mission, and we are desperately trying to cooperate in such a way that you become pleased.

All these activities are riddled with obstacles that repeatedly test us to the core of who we are and try to break apart whatever we have worked to establish. Māyā is very strong, and we are in the heart of her kingdom. She is expert in using our own deepest fears against us and manipulating our false egos in such a way as to undermine our devotee relationships. She is determined to make us serious, whether we like it or not. We are all dependent upon your protection and causeless mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Though living and serving in London is blissful, it is not easy. This city is not a fit place for a gentleman, what to speak of ladies. During the day the streets are filled with conditioned souls searching for Kṛṣṇa in all the wrong places, shopping for the temporary pleasures of this world and working so hard to get relief from the entanglements of their own desires. At night the ether is filled with sirens, screams, and pounding music and the air polluted with vile aromas. The conditioned souls degrade themselves to abominable levels with the desperate hope that they may finally achieve a state of consciousness from which they may never return to the dismal, empty lives they lead during the day.

Meanwhile, despite the constant disruptions, we try to rest in preparation for another day of surrendered service to this glorious mission of yours. We are trying to establish a spiritual oasis in the middle of this desertlike city. We are working according to your guidelines to erect a beacon to guide the fallen souls in for a safe landing after their high-flying escapades. We are striving to build a lighthouse in your honor so that the souls lost on the ocean of nescience can safely find the shelter they have been so frantically hunting for.

Space, privacy, and facility are at a premium in our wonderful little building at 10 Soho Street, where distractions, diversions, and dilemmas are a dime a dozen. Still, the brave and glorious Vaiṣṇavas who have taken up your service are rising early and chanting in great ecstasy the glories of the spiritual master, who is saving us all from the blazing fire of material existence. That is *you*, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

The devotees here at your ISKCON hospital are on call from early morning until late at night, admitting many patients in the hope that a few souls may be saved. We just want to render some insignificant service to the lotus feet of our glorious founder-*ācārya*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the devotees here at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara *dhāma* are like soldiers sold out to the mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and they are always giving more and more of themselves in ways that never cease to impress, inspire, and amaze the rest of us. Against all odds, the devotees here are thriving. They are giving their lives in a desperate attempt to repay the debt owed to you, Your Divine Grace.

Thank you for risking your life in the service of your spiritual master. Thank you for saving so many fallen conditioned souls, whom you did not even know. Thank you for staying up late to translate your books. Thank you for taking time to write so many letters. Thank you for traveling so much even though you were so sick. Thank you for taking chances and risks on behalf of the Supreme Lord. And thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for not giving up on us!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we marvel at your pastimes. How determined, bold, courageous, and relentless you were in bringing the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu to the West! Now look at what you have accomplished and continue to accomplish. You started a worldwide spiritual revolution, and the war wages on. We are on the front lines, and we don’t want to be summertime soldiers or sunshine patriots who shrink from their duties in times of crisis. You are our *saṅkīrtana* general, and we just want to be your instruments on the battlefield. We want to glorify you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for all that you have given this world.

You have blessed us all in so many ways, with so many gifts of love and support.  
YOU have given us the PROCESS — hearing and chanting.  
YOU have given us the MISSION — preach in every town and village.  
YOU have given us the REASON — compassion.  
YOU have given us the OPPORTUNITY — to go back to Godhead.  
YOU have given us KṚṢṆA — the all-attractive Supreme Personality of Godhead.  
How can we ever repay you for this, Śrīla Prabhupāda?

In the mood of Śrīla Mādhavendra Purī, we do not allow our minds to budge an inch from the determination to repay our debt by serving the lotus feet of our founder-*ācārya*, His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, though we are unable to do it.

You are our qualification, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and we are completely dependent upon you. You are always here with us in ISKCON. Never was there a time when you were not with us, and never will there be a time when you are not right here in the center, directing and guiding the saving grace of humanity at large.

You are the *jagat-guru*, savior of the whole universe, and we here at ISKCON London fall down in the dust at your lotus feet and offer repeated obeisances, with pleas of desperation to serve you in this glorious mission. Please engage us in your service, life after life, and protect us from the onslaughts of Māyā. We need your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara Dhāma, ISKCON London (Soho), UK.  
(written by Jai Nitāi Dāsa)

## Longdenville

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our respectful prostrated obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace on this glorious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā!

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

About five hundred years ago, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu desired that His name be spread to every town and village in the world, and He predicted that it would happen.

*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*

**“In every town and village, the chanting of My name will be heard.” (*****Caitanya-bhāgavata*****,** ***Antya-khaṇḍa*** **4.126)**

For the last several generations, Vaiṣṇavas wondered what about the meaning of that verse — how the teachings of Lord Caitanya could be accepted by the *mlecchas* and *yavanas*. Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura longed for the day when people from all classes, countries, and creeds would come together at the holy city of Māyāpur under the banner of Lord Caitanya and chant the holy names, and he predicted that the person who would fulfill this prophecy would soon appear on this planet. Then, following in his footsteps, your spiritual master, His Divine Grace Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, strongly desired to preach Lord Caitanya’s message in the Western world, and he ordered you and some of your godbrothers to take up that responsibility. However, you are the one who fulfilled that prophecy of Lord Caitanya, the prediction of Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura, and the inner desire of your spiritual master, who on his appearance day in 1936 quoted from a poem you wrote as proof that you understood his mission. He read:

Absolute is sentient  
Thou hast proved,  
Impersonal calamity  
Thou hast moved.

You perfectly imbibed and followed the instructions of your spiritual master. You were blessed by him and the previous *ācāryas*, and you were chosen by Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu to fulfill His desire to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world. What more proof do we need of your unique position and exalted status as a *rasika* Vaiṣṇava? Only a Vaiṣṇava who is empowered can spread the holy names of Kṛṣṇa all over the world, and only a Vaiṣṇava who has a personal relationship with Kṛṣṇa can strike the spark of love of Kṛṣṇa in the hearts of *mlecchas* and *yavanas* in the way you did and turn that spark into a flame. In spite of your unprecedented miraculous achievements, you remained so humble that you never took any personal credit but instead would always say that it was all by the grace of your guru mahārāja and Kṛṣṇa, and that everything happens by their combined mercy.

In the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Ādi* 1.58), Śrīla Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja Gosvāmī states:

*jīve sākṣāt nāhi tāte guru caittya-rūpe*  
*śikṣā-guru haya kṛṣṇa-mahānta-svarūpe*

**“Since one cannot visually experience the presence of the Supersoul, He appears before us as a liberated devotee. Such a spiritual master is none other than Kṛṣṇa Himself.”**

*śikṣā-guruke ta’ jāni kṛṣṇera svarūpa*  
*antaryāmī, bhakta-śreṣṭha,—ei dui rūpa*

**“One should know the instructing spiritual master to be the Personality of Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa manifests Himself as the Supersoul and as the greatest devotee of the Lord.” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi*** **1.47)**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the *śikṣā-guru* for all of us and for all those who will be so fortunate to come in contact with you through your books for the next ten thousand years. Even in your physical absence, through your books and lectures, and through your faithful and dedicated disciples who repeat your words, you continue to bless us with your mercy. Thank you for all that you have done for us, for all that you are doing, and for all that you will continue to do for us. We are eternally indebted to you, and although we can never repay you, may we take advantage of your merciful association so that our little sparks will someday burst into blazing fires of loving devotional service to Kṛṣṇa. By your grace we can become qualified to really assist you in preserving the legacy you left and in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness here in Trinidad, this little island in the Caribbean that you considered important.

In your letters you wrote:

Long ago I was invited by the people of Trinidad but at that time I could not go, although I had great desire to go there. Now you are fulfilling my desire. \[Letter to Vaikuṇṭhanātha and Śāradiā, 4 April 1971\]

You can immediately secure that land, and I am asking Saurabha from Vrindaban to send you the sample plans exactly like our Vrindaban temple. When the time is ripe, I shall be very glad come there and open the temple.

Krishna may bless you because you are doing so much for Krishna. Try to have a nice temple for Krishna at Trinidad. It is an important place. \[Letter to Citsukhānanda, 14 July 1974\]

We are encouraged that out of your compassion you thought of Trinidad and that you desired to come here. Although you never personally came, you nevertheless sent your representatives and you came and stayed with us through your instructions. So on this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we pray to understand your vision for spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness here. We pray to better appreciate who you are, your presence among us, and the gift of Kṛṣṇa consciousness you gave us. May we show our gratitude and love the way you desired us to: by cooperating to keep your ISKCON together, not as just another nominal spiritual institution but as a house in which the whole world can live together in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Your servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha Mandir, Longdenville, Trinidad.  
(written by Agni Dāsa)

## Manokwari (Indonesia)

All glories to Śrī Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga!  
All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Before beginning our offering, let us offer our most humble obeisances unto His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, with great compassion you are giving the opportunity to fallen souls like us to engage in devotional service. You are the spiritual master of the whole world. You are full of mercy, and are thus uplifting us although we are so fallen that we are lower than a blade of grass.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us the strength to always nicely serve the mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, which is to spread the teachings of Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world. Although we are not qualified to assist you, please help us be small instruments in your mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

We devotees who are serving here at Manokwari very much need your causeless mercy. We are fallen souls — not very strong and without good intelligence. Although we do not understand so much about your mission, we pray that you may kindly allow us to always engage in your devotional service.

We thus end our humble offering and pray for your mercy.

From the servants of the servants of the servants of your servants,

The devotees at Śrī Rādhā-Manohara Āśrama, Manokwari, Indonesia.  
(written by Śuci Dāsa)

## Mauritius

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, last year we observed the thirtieth anniversary of your visit to Mauritius. In October of 1975 you blessed this small island by placing your lotus feet upon it. You then predicted that it would become the first Vaiṣṇava country on this planet.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, since that time two important temples, namely the Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma Mandir and the Rādhā-Golokānanda Mandir, have emerged. Various smaller centers have also taken birth. Your ISKCON, which is an expansion of your own body, is the institution that now attracts the largest crowds during its activities, especially during Kṛṣṇa’s Janmāṣṭamī festival.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, there is no systematic preaching on the island, but still it is amazing to observe an increasing number of devotees year after year. We have gone through so many difficult and seemingly insurmountable incidents, in addition to management conflicts. But still your prediction gradually coming true in some inexplicable manner.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are conscious of your presence all over the world and of your being the captain to guide us through the ten thousand golden years. But we also feel that Your Divine Grace has a special interest in the conditioned souls living on this island. For this we have no words to describe how much we are indebted to Your Divine Grace. Please continue to bestow your causeless mercy upon us.

Your most fallen servants at ISKCON Mauritius.  
(written by Mādana Gopāla Dāsa)

## Melbourne

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, who has opened my eyes, blinded by the darkness of ignorance, with the torchlight of knowledge.**

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**

**Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda

Devotees often say to me how unfortunate I am that I didn’t meet you, since I joined so soon after your departure. I’m afraid I don’t feel that way. Please let me explain why.

I feel I meet you every day. It is my habit to listen to your lectures every morning when I rise and take bath. On returning to my room to dress and apply my *tilaka,* I listen to your MP3 cd’s. It gives me great pleasure to hear you preach the simple and sublime philosophy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

On my last visit to Vṛndāvana, the devotees asked me to give the morning *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* class. After the class I met Dhanañjaya Prabhu in the MVT gardens. He thanked me for the class and told me that the class was proof that your books would be the law books for the next ten thousand years. I responded that if I had said anything it was only what I had gleaned from studying your books. I had spent a couple of hours studying your purports the night before. He replied that that was exactly his point: “All we have to do is repeat in our own words what we have read from Śrīla Prabhupāda, and we will be successful.”

This is why I feel I know you. In fact, you state this principle in the dedication to your guru mahārāja at the beginning of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*: “He lives forever by his divine instructions, and the follower lives with him.”

I endeavor to study your books every day. I am amazed at what I find. The benefits are many:

You teach me how to see with the vision of a *mahā-bhāgavata*.  
You teach me how to practice Kṛṣṇa consciousness.  
You teach me how simple it is to remember Kṛṣṇa.  
You present Kṛṣṇa in such an easy-to-understand way.  
You yourself said, “If you want to know me, read my books.”

Every day I read or hear your lectures, I feel I am with you. When I follow your instructions, I am with you.

As a result, I find myself becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious. You are the transparent medium acting like a telescope that brings a distant object up close.

This year we are teaching the Bhakti-śāstrī course, which is in its third year here, and the Bhakti-vaibhava course, which is in its second year.

I see how enthusiastic the devotees become as they systematically study your books. They mature and become enthused to preach.

Thank you for this opportunity to know you and to know Kṛṣṇa and his devotees. Thank you for the opportunity to present and share this process with your devotees.

We pray to continue this service.

Your servants at ISKCON Melbourne, Australia.  
(written by Aniruddha Dāsa)

## Miami

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you!

For the eternal spiritual benefit of mankind you established the International Society for Krishna Consciousness in 1966. While the movement was still very young, you instructed us in no uncertain terms that you expected us to attain and maintain the highest standard of purity, self-restraint, morality, and obedience to your instructions. You taught us that Kali-yuga is an ocean of faults and that people in general are quickly and unavoidably succumbing to the degrading influence of this age.

However, you have blessed us with full protection from that degradation to the degree that we strictly follow the path of Kṛṣṇa consciousness as you taught it. In *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (1.18.6) Sūta Gosvāmī says, “The very day and moment the Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, left this earth, the personality of Kali, who promotes all kinds of irreligious activities, came into this world.” The personality of Kali influences only those who engage in irreligious activities. It is very significant that you demanded that everyone seriously practicing Kṛṣṇa consciousness must daily chant sixteen rounds of the Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra and also strictly follow the four regulative principles: strictly abstaining in every way from eating meat, fish, or eggs, from having illicit sex, from using any kind of intoxicant, including coffee, tea, and cigarettes, and from gambling.

In the strict sense, we are also vulnerable to the influence of Kali to the degree that we engage in any unauthorized form of sense gratification. We are attracted to material sense gratification to the extent that we fall short of offenseless chanting. And to the degree that we fall short of offenseless chanting , we also fall short in our ability to effectively spread the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement all over the world, as you have done.

We sometimes tend to give more emphasis to examples in your life that we feel we can interpret as justifying our sense gratification than we do to those examples that clearly instruct us how to advance in spiritual life and propagate this movement. For example, one of your traveling secretaries used to send regular reports to the temples about your bold preaching all over the world. Once he mentioned, as an insignificant side note, that you once drank a small serving of a soda as a digestive aid. But as soon as word got out, some devotees exclaimed, “It’s authorized!”“It’s bona fide!”“Śrīla Prabhupāda is the *ācārya*, so whatever he does is what he wants us to do!” Within a short time, one could find sodas in many of our temples, although they had previously been forbidden. Blinded by the desire for sense gratification, we minimized the rest of the report, which was intended to inspire us with the desire to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness everywhere.

You also warned us, “When one eats food offered by a materialistic man, one’s mind becomes contaminated, and when the mind is contaminated, one is unable to think of Kṛṣṇa properly.” (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Antya* 6.278)

Still, in so many ways we justify eating pizza, ice cream, restaurant and airplane meals, and many other foods prepared by materialists. Despite your warning, we speculate that it actually has no bad influence on us. The problem is that although our mind becomes more wicked or materialistic by eating that food, we don’t realize how much and in what ways we are being hurt by eating that food. This is because our intelligence has also been influenced by our materialistic mind, thus reducing our ability to analyze the situation in proper perspective. Many times we even break some of the regulative principles or fail to chant our rounds, feel justified in doing so because we perceive our situation with clouded intelligence. Thus we allow the personality of Kali to enter our lives and hinder our chances for spiritual advancement and our potency for spreading this great preaching mission.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, this year marks four decades since you established the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement in America, but it has been only in the last few years that our Society has become actively involved in formally training and educating the members of this movement outside of the temple *āśrama*.

Here at ISKCON Miami our mood is to develop our temple in such a way that it will be more suitable and conducive for the younger generation of devotees to take over. We are well aware that those presently dedicated to managing and maintaining this temple will not live forever, and we do not wish to hold on to our posts, like materialistic politicians, until the fag end of our lives. It is our hope to gradually hand over our temple to the more qualified hands of the young devotees born into this movement. We want to hand over not a temple plagued by major problems but a well-organized dynamic temple with an active and supportive congregation, flourishing Deity program, and well-established educational and training programs. The educational and training programs would include a full-scale *varṇāśrama* college to teach *śāstric* education, Deity worship, and courses on many other practical fields of knowledge, so that the present and future generations of ISKCON devotees will have the best opportunities for practicing spiritual life while fulfilling their duties in the material society. In other words, we are working toward the establishment of *varṇāśrama* for the benefit of ISKCON and, ultimately, the whole world.

You have given us everything we need to become pure devotees of Lord Kṛṣṇa in this life time and to guide the world along that path. But we eternally have free will to choose to accept or reject the good instructions you have given us. We understand that although at present many of us cannot strictly follow your instructions due to weak-heartedness, we will gradually attain spiritual perfection as long as we remain favorably connected to this movement. There is no loss or diminution. Please bless us with the ability and desire to choose your pure spiritual instructions over sense gratification so that we can become fully empowered to help spread the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement all over the world.

Your humble servants at ISKCON Miami, Florida, USA.

## Mumbai (Chowpatty)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Your wonderful teachings, books, and mercy shower profusely on our tiny Chowpatty temple, known internationally as “the simple temple.”

We are your spiritual grandchildren and always have a very special relationship with you. We can feel your very presence and blessings, which you profusely shower on us as we perform all our endeavors.

Under the able guidance of your spiritual son Rādhānātha Swami, our guru mahārāja, and many of his godbrothers and godsisters, we are simultaneously missing you and experiencing your presence.

You will be happy to know that Food for Life is going on so strongly and with such widespread effect that children of Muslim, Christian, Dalit, and other backgrounds in the municipal schools are enjoying sumptuous *prasādam* every day. We have received endless praise for this program, even from chief ministers and other senior government officials. For your pleasure, on your Vyāsa-pūjā day we will show the latest VCD prepared on this subject.

Preaching, which was so dear to your heart, is proceeding at a galloping pace in various schools, colleges, and universities, attracting young students away from bad habits and *anarthas*. Thousands of students have become devotees of Lord Kṛṣṇa, chanting sixteen rounds daily and following four regulative principles.

By your mercy we have started the Bhaktivedanta Academy for Culture and Education (BACE), which offers to eager students courses in the *bhakti-śāstras*, as well as other courses that will help them progress in their spiritual lives. The number of eager, enthusiastic *brahmacārīs,* now totaling eighty-four, is ever increasing.

To expand the original orphanage (LNHO), we have acquired a large piece of land outside Mumbai, at Wada. There the children not only can get a regular education but also enjoy a highly sattvic rural atmosphere with cows, where they can learn weaving and other cottage industries. At the same time, they will progress spiritually in an undisturbed, healthy atmosphere. This program is for Marathi-speaking students only. The English-speaking students will remain at Chowpatty.

Another new achievement has been Govinda’s Restaurant, which has become extremely popular because of the superexcellent vision and training imparted by Rādhānātha Mahārāja. The high quality of the *prasādam* has attracted many new people to our temple.

By your kind blessings, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, and Śrī Gopālnāthjī now have an even more effulgent temple decor, which attracts so many more people on some festival days that we have to use large movie screens outside the temple. In this way, even people walking on the street can benefit from the temple program.

With our most sincere gratitude,  
Your servants at ISKCON’s Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha Mandir, Chowpatty, Mumbai, India.  
(written by Śrī Nāthjī Dāsa)

## Mumbai (Juhu)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

ISKCON Juhu has been one of your special babies, and devotees here are fortunate to live at Hare Krishna Land, where you spent so much time and struggled so hard to build this palace for Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsabihārījī.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you came to this material world and shed your blood, tears, and sweat to form ISKCON, through which you injected people with a dose of *kṛṣṇa-bhakti*. Truly a sacrifice unparallel. Thankless task. You have changed our lives. Like camels, we were tasting our own blood through sense gratification, and you gave us Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsabihārīs *caraṇāmṛta* instead, and we are slowly relishing Their *caraṇāmṛta* and giving up sucking our own blood.

You always made big plans, and the resources for your plans just came in by your potency. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you wanted ISKCON Juhu to do something unique. The Second Phase Project is nearing completion. We pray that with your blessings we can multiply our preaching by making full use of the added infrastructure.

Our love for you can only be shown by how well we cooperate to further this movement. So please bless us so that all the wonderful devotees here at ISKCON Mumbai will give up any vestiges of petty-mindedness and work together to fulfill your dream. You have shed gallons of blood for us, so we pray that by your blessings we will shed our individual egos and petty thinking processes to fulfill your instructions.

Your servants at ISKCON Juhu, Mumbai, India.

## Nagpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

While you were in Hyderabad in 1976, you made plans to send your disciples to go and preach in Nagpur. They came to Nagpur and planted the first seed, and later Mr. Ashis Roy from Nagpur wrote you a letter asking you to open a center in Nagpur. Somehow or other this center did not open.

You wrote back to Mr. Roy:

The process of opening a center somewhere is that we go there and hold classes for one week on the teachings of Bhagavad-gita and if the people like, then we’ll open a center. Actually, the teachings of Sri Caitanya Mahaprabhu that in every town and village there will be a center of Krishna Consciousness, is essential. People must co-operate. If not, then they will have to suffer. . . . I would like to take this opportunity to invite you to our Vrndavana festival which will be held on the 20th of April up to the 27th of April. We will be opening our temple there. If you can come, then we can talk in detail about a center at Nagpur. \[Letter to Mr. Ashis Roy, 8 April 1975\]

Nagpur has a big scope for spreading Kṛṣṇa conscious. It is the second capital of Maharashtra, with an international airport. It is an important city that is part of Vidarbha, which was ruled by Maharaja Bhīṣmaka, the father of Rukmiṇī. Lord Kṛṣṇa personally came to Vidarbha, and Lord Rāmacandra also blessed the area while on His way to Daṇḍakāraṇya Forest. Nagpur is also famous for its oranges, and once a Nagpuri offered you a big basket full of them.

Hari Śauri to Śrīla Prabhupāda: Mr. Gupta just sent a big basket full of oranges from Nagpur. \[Conversation on train to Allahabad, 11 January 1977\]

After the first program held in Nagpur, in 1976, the preaching died down and was resumed in 1980. Then in 1984 a center was established. Since then the preaching has gone through some difficulties, with the temple moving from one place to another. For many years our main objective was to secure a nice piece of land and fulfill your expressed desire for a beautiful temple here.

We have always heard from His Holiness Lokanāth Swami how you said that an ISKCON temple has to be established in all the major cities of India, and how you especially pointed three times to Nagpur on a map and said, “This is the central part of India, and so a temple must be here.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your causeless mercy, this year, on an auspicious day in the month of Caitra (March 30), we successfully performed a Bhūmi-pūjā. Today ISKCON Nagpur has an acre of land that has a road on three sides, and bordering the northeast is a major road eighty feet across —something very auspicious according to *vastu-śāstra*. This plot is also near Asia’s biggest grain market and the wealthiest community in Nagpur; it is called the Kuvera Nagari. We have also succeeded in naming the location of the new land Prabhupāda Town, and the main road Bhaktivedanta Marg. We plan to call this project “The Glory of Central India.” To cultivate the area surrounding the temple, we are planning to move nearby soon. A pious member of our congregation has given us sixteen rooms in an old apartment complex just ten meters in front of the newly acquired land. In this way, after twenty-two years of the history of ISKCON Nagpur, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha have finally gotten Their own place. Soon we will select four of the most qualified architects in the area to design the future temple. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bestow your special mercy and give us the strength so that your dream come true.

As for our preaching, for the Sunday program we often have new speakers from different places. Nagpur is a large educational area, and there is a golden future for youth preaching here. The city has one regional engineering college. Recently, two big youth festivals were staged, in which hundreds of youth participated. The preaching is going on in government medical colleges, agricultural colleges, and a few engineering colleges. In Nagpur the young devotee ladies are also very active. A few have earned Bhakti-śāstrī degrees from the Vṛndāvana Institute of Higher Education and are training other young ladies. The young ladies are also very actively and enthusiastically preaching, doing weekly *sat-saṅgas* in different areas. The congregational devotees, especially the ladies, are very active in helping the temple with preaching and in attending the morning program and giving classes. The temple devotees are also getting good training in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

There are a lot of things to be done in Nagpur to build up the preaching. There is a great need to take more care of the devotees and the congregation — to develop a structure that can care for the devotees, attract educated people, and stress how it is so important to increase one’s taste for chanting and hearing *kṛṣṇa-kathā*. We need leadership that can unite everyone so that the members support each other and dedicate their lives to the mission out of devotion for Lord Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda as a Nagpuri once offered you a big basket of oranges, we would also like to make an offering that can please you. We therefore pray for your blessings on the devotees of Nagpur, so that the seed of *bhakti* may bear fruit. We pray to you for devotion and service. We pray that we can forget our selfish differences and work together for the benefit of both ourselves and others by helping people attain this rare gift of Kṛṣṇa consciousness you have given us.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, all the devotees of ISCKON Nagpur greet you on this auspicious day, the 110thanniversary of your birth, and we hope that your name and fame will be spread throughout every corner of this city of Nagpur.

The devotees of Nagpur, India.

## Nava Nīlācala

Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your holy lotus feet. All glories to Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga!

Words can hardly express the gratitude we feel toward you for everything you have done for us. We feel that no words can, even nearly, describe your glories. But still, we want to try to glorify you.

Reading about your pastimes on this planet, which have been described by your disciples who witnessed them, we learn how you were always caring, compassionate, and full of all the good qualities of a Vaiṣṇava. Situated in the highest consciousness, you always desired to spread the glories of Lord Kṛṣṇa and deliver the fallen souls. When we read your books and try to understand them properly, the propensity for material life and material values gets diminished. You give us faith and courage in myriad ways. One of the devotees who lives here wrote to you:

Sometimes in my life, Śrīla Prabhupāda, I feel that you are so vividly present and that you are leading us all on this way of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Thank you for being so merciful and for personally looking after this great movement. It is said that the grandfather is more merciful than the father, and you are our grandfather, who with a lot of patience lead forward his grandchildren.

I remember how once I had made *sandeśa* for my guru mahārāja, and I wanted so much that he would like it. But when he tasted it I saw him make an unpleasant facial expression, as if he didn’t like the *sandeśa* I’d made. I was desperate and started to helplessly pray very intensely to you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, depending on your mercy. The very next moment my guru mahārāja’s face turned into a big smile, and I knew that this was your mercy. Thank you.

Another devotee wrote how she and her devotee friend were caring late at night for a third devotee who was in trouble, providing everything that the troubled devotee needed. It was after midnight when she came home to rest. She had been working at her job the whole morning, and the rest of the day she had spent caring for the troubled devotee. Tired, she went to sleep, and immediately you came to her in a dream, Śrīla Prabhupāda. She explained later that you were looking very beautiful and were shining with transcendental light. She was looking at you and you said, “Thank you very much for taking care of my ISCKON. If you take care of others, you will also receive care and help.”

When she woke up she knew that you are very thankful when you see that we care for other devotees and that you personally want to see that we care for everyone in ISKCON, even for the “smallest” devotee, who might not be someone special or someone with the high standards.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for directing us to mutually care for each other. Since you are His pure devotee, Kṛṣṇa wants to serve you, but you don’t allow Him to because you want to serve Him. But to us you freely grant permission to serve you and your mission. Therefore we think if we serve you, Kṛṣṇa will be very pleased with us. Please make us more aware of this fact and let us develop deep faith and loving attachment for Kṛṣṇa’s holy name.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants in Nava Nīlācala, Pula, Croatia.

## New Biharvan

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to you on this most auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā.

**Celebrating Forty Years of ISKCON**

Five hundred years ago, Lord Caitanya predicted that His name would be chanted in every town and village throughout the world:

*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*  
*\[CB Antya-khaṇḍa 4.126\]*

Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura initiated the process of fulfilling Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s desire by writing books in English and predicting the arrival of a commander in chief who would spread the holy name throughout the world. In 1936, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī amplified the desire of Lord Caitanya and Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura by giving the most visionary and revolutionary instruction in the history of Vaiṣṇavism: he asked you to go to the West and preach in English. That instruction became a formal reality with the incorporation of ISKCON in New York in 1966. Now, in 2006, we celebrate the fortieth anniversary of this momentous event.

The strength of the spiritual master’s instruction is evident by the fact that in less than forty years “Hare Kṛṣṇa” has become a household word throughout the world. With more than four hundred centers and farm communities and thousands of dedicated followers, ISKCON has enjoyed a success that is truly a historical phenomenon. This success is the fructification of your intense desire to please your guru mahārāja, the *paramparā,* and Lord Caitanya.

You further amplified the instruction of your guru mahārāja by asking us to distribute your books. “There is no doubt about it, to distribute books is our most important activity.” (Letter to Rāmeśvara Dāsa, 3 August 1973)

The strength of your instruction now carries all of us and sustains us. Your disciples, granddisciples, and great-granddisciples are nurtured by distributing millions of books in English and other languages and spreading Lord Caitanya’s glories to every nook and corner of the world. There are now devotees and temples in every major city and not-so-major city, such as Boise. This is the proof of the power of your books. Behind the success of every temple and every devotee, there is a book, delivered by a dedicated servant of yours.

**Celebrating Twenty Years in New Biharvan, Boise**

One small example of the power of your books is your Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Baṅkebihārī temple in Boise, Idaho. This year we are celebrating our twentieth anniversary. These twenty years have been the story of how *one* book can change the course of someone’s life, and how a few dedicated devotees who sincerely take those instructions to heart can change the landscape of a city. By your causeless mercy, the tree of Kṛṣṇa consciousness now grows and flourishes in Boise, the city of trees.

You referred to your books as time bombs: they sit in people’s homes and offices waiting to go off, and when they do, the impact is phenomenal. The Boise temple is the result of once such time bomb. A traveling Indian student received *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* at the St. Louis Airport in 1973 for a donation of fifty cents. The *Gītā* adorned his bookshelf in the living room for eight years before it was finally opened by a pregnant mother in Chicago in 1981. After she had read it for three straight days, things were never the same for her. Following her husband to Boise, she opened a temple with small brass Gaura-Nitāi deities on a coffee table in a converted garage. To Their Lordships’ right was a small, smiling photo of Your Divine Grace. This was October of 1986.

In the decades that followed, we received the attentive care and love of some of your dear sons—His Holiness Gaṇapati Swami, Gopāl Krishna Goswami, Hanumatpresaka Swami, and Kamalānanda Prabhu. They encouraged us through our difficulties, gave us guidance to improve, and sustained us with their Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We owe a deep debt of gratitude to them.

The temple now serves as a spiritual home for a small but vibrant community of your granddisciples, who have been inspired by your books and have imbibed the instructions within them. They perform innumerable *sevās* for Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Baṅkebihārī. Here are only a few. Please accept them as an offering at your lotus feet.

Aja Govinda Prabhu for the temple hall cleaning  
Amṛta Devī Dāsī for the Sunday School and grounds cleanup  
Aruddhā Devī Dāsī for Deity dressing, *prasādam,* and *saṅkīrtana*  
Balabhadra Prabhu for fire sacrifices and *abhiṣekas*  
Chandra Prabhu for the prison program and counseling  
Gopāl Hari Prabhu for Ratha-yātrās and *saṅkīrtana*  
Lalitā Devī Dāsī for temple cleaning and the Food for Life program  
Lakṣmī Devī Dāsī for play props and decoration  
Mādhurya Devī Dāsī for gardening and organization  
Mālatī Devī Dāsī for cleaning and organization and assisting other devotees  
Pāñcālī Devī Dāsī for making Deity jewelry  
Rādhikā Ramaṇa Prabhu for overseeing temple construction and PR  
Saṅgītā Devī Dāsī for making *maṅgala* sweets  
Śrī Hari Prabhu for kitchen cleaning and dressing you  
Śrīnivāsa Prabhu for dressing you, maintaining the website, and kitchen cleaning  
Śyāma-sakhī Devī Dāsī for *maṅgala* sweets and jewelry-room *sevā*  
Tulasī Kṛṣṇa Devī Dāsī for Śrīmati Tulasi Devī*sevā*  
Upendra Prabhu for making Deity garlands and *prasādam* distribution

These devotees in turn have each engaged many congregation members to serve you and Śrī Śrī Rādhā Baṅkebihārī. Most importantly, the devotees are making their children Kṛṣṇa conscious. The Boise temple has focused on your desire to raise children as devotees of Kṛṣṇa. Almost twenty children have gone or are going through Kṛṣṇa conscious home-schooling. The most important aspect of this system is that your books—*Bhagavad-gītā As It Is, Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, Kṛṣṇa* book, and your *Līlāmṛta*—are the basis of the curriculum.

All glories to you and to your books!

Please continue to protect us and shelter us under your lotus feet.

The devotees in New Biharvan, Boise, USA.  
(written by Anantarūpa Dāsa)

## New Dvārakā

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you on this most auspicious of all auspicious days!

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

You timely blessed this material world by appearing in this fallen Age of Kali to save us and all the rest of the conditioned souls as well. We are very fortunate to be engaged here at your New Dvārakā*dhāma,* ISKCON Los Angeles. This is your Western world headquarters, and we are inspired by meditating on your lifelong example to serve the orders of your spiritual master with undeviating, uninterrupted, unmotivated, and unrivaled devotional service. You did everything simply for his pleasure. Similarly, the book distribution, the *prasādam* distribution, the Deity worship, and the *harināma* the devotees here in New Dvārakā engage in are just some of the services enthusiastically performed for your continual glorification. The New Dvārakā community remains absorbed in your intense desire for the temple to be managed this way.
