We pray to do more as we advance and realize that this is our link to your ecstatic association, your merciful glance, and your affectionate embrace.

Engaged in service from early until it is late,  
In this way we pray to meet you again someday at Kṛṣṇa’s gate.  
You always served Śrī Kṛṣṇa with all your might;  
The example we needed, the bona fide spiritual master’s sight.  
On the path of *bhakti* embracing transcendental bliss,  
Real life to be sure, absolutely nothing amiss.  
Free from the miseries and suffering of the material world,  
A life so ecstatic more valuable than diamonds and emeralds.  
But we are lazy and unfortunate, and have many more flaws.  
This Māyā knows and, with temptation, draws  
Our attention elsewhere to phantasmagoria and mesmerization.  
Material enjoyments distract us from our spiritual master’s mission.  
We are bereft of transcendence and do not know what to do or say,  
But if we keep following your instructions it will surely happen anyway.  
By following the regulative principles and remaining pure,  
We know everything spiritual and material will flourish, for sure.  
Lord Caitanya instructed, “Chant, chant, chant!”  
But our causeless unwillingness says, “Can’t, can’t, can’t!”  
This is the only way, the only way, the only way:  
To advance in Kali-yuga and to not stray  
From the path given to us by you, our teacher;  
To practice with enthusiasm and to be a preacher.  
Do not retire to some so-called comfortable life;  
There is no such thing in this material world of strife.  
We must spread this ISKCON movement far and wide;  
Then when we see you we can stand in pride  
That we maintained your mission and did not let it fall:  
“Do this at least” was almost your last call.  
Such a great movement Lord Caitanya started,  
And you worked relentlessly focused until you departed,  
So that everyone can be blessed with Kṛṣṇa’s mercy  
And go back home, back to Godhead — Kṛṣṇa’s abode of eternal ecstasy!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us all on this most auspicious of all auspicious days, so that we may never deviate from your lotus feet and may continually please you with all our activities. Please keep inspiring us to continue your mission and increase it more and more.And lastly, thank you for giving us pure Kṛṣṇa consciousness, without additions, subtractions, or adulterations.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*(jaya) śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya prabhu nityānanda*  
*śrī-advaita gadādhara śrīvāsādi-gaura-bhakta-vṛnda*

*Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/*  
*Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare*

Your servants at New Dvārakā dhāma, Los Angeles, California, USA.

## New Gokula

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our eternal well-wisher. From the depths of our hearts, we want to thank you for saving us and for giving our lives their real purpose. We never cease to wonder by what miracle we have all somehow come into your association. From the moment we met you, through your sincere followers, we became the most fortunate of souls as you poured your miracles of mercy into our barren lives. Everything good and beautiful in our lives has all come to us as your many matchless gifts. We may never truly realize the full scope of all your gifts to the world.

Yet one thing we know for sure: you are the most amazing instrument of Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s kindness, empowered to offer all mankind His divine gifts of salvation through the *yuga-dharma* of Śrī Kṛṣṇa *saṅkīrtana*. And this expanding miracle of *gaura-vāṇī* has incarnated on your own very lips. Hearing the Absolute Truth from your glorious books and classes has now allowed us all to desire entrance into that sacred realm of pure devotion where you eternally reside. And we pray to become much more attached to the life-giving nectar flowing like a fountain of youth from your divine lips. Only in that shower of pure nectar can we hope to be forever cleansed of all our *anarthas* and eventually be blessed to regain our taste for the Lord’s eternal service.

To quote your deep realizations (from your Vyāsa-pūjā offering of 1936 to your own great master): “The divine message from his holy lips is the congenial thing for suffering humanity.” Like you, we are determined to continue the highest welfare work of offering as many souls as possible the shelter of your perfect message. Then they may at least know something of their real identity and thus hopefully also become saved from Kali’s onslaught.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you dedicated your entire life’s energy toward this purpose, and your success continues to manifest even in spite of our severe imperfections and lack of deep unity. This is all somewhat mysterious yet so glorious — how you can work your miracles of mercy even through us, your inept but grateful instruments. We pray never to leave the sun of your association, so that we can continue to be rewarded by these awesome glimpses into the ineffable beauty and opulence of a true lover of Kṛṣṇa, one of His very own. We also pray to always remain alert to the delusions that could steal us away from your perfect and complete shelter.

If we can somehow be allowed the constant remembrance of your infinite love for all souls, we will remain somewhat useful to you and thus be allowed to further witness your endless miracles expanding all over the world. This gives us enduring joy and hope, and this divine legacy of your never-ending gifts remains our lives’ only sure solace.

Your grateful although unworthy servants in the New Gokula community, ISKCON Vancouver, Canada.  
(written by Gokulānanda Dāsa)

## New Goloka

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We, the inhabitants of New Goloka, bow down together to offer you our humble and heartfelt obeisances. We pray that Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Golokānanda will empower us insignificant souls to glorify you properly on this holy anniversary of your appearance in the world.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda! May victory be yours! O master, our eternal well-wisher and shelter, please accept our prostrate obeisances at your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we form an unlikely congregation. By age, sex, nationality, social class, education, and occupation we are separated. We have no common politics or economic interests to bind us together. In ordinary matters, our opinions and convictions diverge widely and clash often. Our upbringing and even our old religious backgrounds forcefully divide us. Above all, we are the typical unlucky children of our time, the wretched, quarrelsome, sinful, cacophonous Age of Kali.

How is it possible, therefore, for us ever to find any common cause at all? How is it possible for us even to tolerate one another, what to speak of love one another? Yet, here we are — chanting, dancing, and praying together, taking shelter together, honoring *kṛṣṇa-prasādam* together, experiencing bliss and enlightenment together, planning and preaching together, going back to Godhead together.

Your ISKCON has united and delivered us all.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we earnestly pray for the transcendental insight needed to properly understand your miraculous creation. This International Society for Krishna Consciousness is no ordinary institution. Although superficially it resembles the mundane corporate structures of our times, ISKCON is no mere legal and organizational convenience. It is nothing like the transitory social conglomerations that sometimes appear around sectarian causes, brand loyalties, or charismatic leaders.

ISKCON is the manifestation of an ancient prophetic vision. It is the nascent fruit of the preaching, spiritual ingenuity, and sacrifice of luminous *ācāryas* and pure devotees reaching far back in our line. ISKCON is a fully transcendental creation that embodies the essential spirit of the *saṅkīrtana* movement and the highest transcendental truth. It is the dwelling place of ŚrīPañca-tattva and is meant for nothing less than the salvation of the entire world.

Of course, the ultimate architect of this sublime institution and the power behind its appearance in the world is the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. But we know that the Almighty Lord is most pleased when his confidential devotee is glorified. Thus, at Kurukṣetra the Lord arranged everything on the battlefield to enhance the fame and glory of his dear friend Arjuna.

Similarly, Lord Caitanya has now elevated you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, above all others by giving you the singular and perpetual honor of being the founder-*ācārya* of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness. Thus, now and forever, your articles of incorporation, your managerial arrangements, your books and teachings, your peerless example, and your last will are enshrined as the highest authority for this transcendental society.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are forever indebted to you for having given us this great field of action, ISKCON. Now please bless us with the necessary spiritual courage and determination to defend and develop your great movement and to keep ourselves safely sheltered within it.

You once remarked, “Without opposition, how can there be glorious victory?” So, whenever opposition, a disturbance, or a threat to ISKCON arises, whether from outside or from within the movement itself, we pray that we may never disappoint you and dishonor ourselves by fleeing the battlefield. During difficult times help us remember that such challenges are arrangements of the Lord. They afford us the priceless opportunity to fight on your behalf. Such struggles in the service of your ISKCON are the very means of our salvation and the source of our ultimate happiness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, may Lord Nṛsiṁhadeva always protect your International Society for Krishna Consciousness so that it may advance from victory to victory. And may every honest soul in the world come forward to assist this transcendental institution and thereby gain the infallible shelter of your lotus feet.

ISKCON founder-*ācārya jagat-guru* Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

Your servants at New Goloka, Hillsborough, North Carolina, USA.

## New Govardhana (Australia)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

You are always present for those who remember you. Hearing their prayers, you accept their service and bless their hearts. How much I wish to be among them! But the truth is I’m always guilty of neglect; not so much in my service, duties, and daily affairs, but more so in my prayerful meditations and memories. It seems that life’s day-to-day affairs have kept me so busy that the time I now spend thinking of you has greatly diminished. It used to be that you were always on my mind. I remember the first time your lotus feet entered my heart. They came in the form of your *Back to Godhead* magazine. I was very young, very foolish, and very proud. The whole phantasmagoric world stood before me, awaiting my decision . . . but, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you mercifully took it all away: my youth, my pride, my foolishness, and whatever else I thought was real.

From that time on, only your books mattered. You appeared to me through your books, for which I had an unquenchable thirst. But as much as I tried, I couldn’t find that many of them, and so I often prayed to the Lord, “Please come to me at least in this way.” Then one day you did appear — in the form of the third volume of the *Kṛṣṇa* book trilogy, and from such an unexpected place: University Library, Adelaide! I was so elated. I turned each page, drinking whatever nectar I could, and then very enthusiastically, and quite shamelessly, I just stole you away (but please forgive me for that). When I found “The Prayers by the Personified *Vedas*” within this book, you became my true philosophical shelter. I would read and re-read, over and over, to the point that very soon my only pleasure came in associating with you.

Gradually I became fortunate enough to collect a few more of your books. I would keep them around the home, hoping that my family members would also benefit. But it wasn’t to be (at least in the short term), and before long I had to protect you from my father. He saw you as a great threat, as the enemy who would one day take away his son. Suddenly, in the midst of a great torrent of abuse, my father picked up your *Nectar of Devotion* and threw it across the room. I couldn’t catch you, so instead, and with much anger, I caught my father. Please forgive us for that, both of us. No one was really hurt, but from then on I kept you hidden away from him.

As I lived at home, the years passed slowly — they seemed like eons. My only possessions were your transcendental books, the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*, and an intense desire to live among the devotees. Sometimes I would bow down, praying for your association, and at times I would just cry. Once, through a very dear friend, I was able to see Your Divine Grace. You came in a most unexpected and wonderful way, but due to my offensive nature and my not knowing how to serve you, you quickly left. I didn’t know how or when your mercy would come again. It appeared to be without cause, though I did suspect that a certain measure of desire influences where and when you appear, and this suspicion has stayed with me throughout life. It was the beginning of wonderful pastimes — pastimes that defy mundane experience, ongoing pastimes that always give testimony to that measure of desire to please you, to serve you, and to please you again.

Sometime later you finally allowed me the privilege of living among the devotees. Within the intimacy of your International Society for Krishna consciousness, your pastimes absolutely enthralled us. They became the object of our attention every day. Since you were fixed on the transcendental platform and in complete Kṛṣṇa consciousness, you were always victorious. No one could defy your preaching prowess. You stood confident and proud in the world, protected by the Lord, who gave success to anyone who would simply serve you. We worked hard to please you, hoping that one day you would visit and bless us with the eternal moments of your divine presence.

And then you came! I vividly remember those nights when I would guard your door, just like an obedient dog. Sometimes you would appear there in passing and show your appreciation. Sometimes you just walked right by. In any case, I remained your obedient dog. You danced in the Ratha-yātrā before Lord Jagannātha, Lord Balarāma, and Subhadrā Devī, enthusing us to do the same, and we would explode in the ecstasy of singing and dancing for your pleasure. At times you would gather us up in your room, where your transcendental smile became our only desired end. When you would gaze upon the form of Lord Gaurāṅga, your moistened eyes gave us true testimony of love for God. Wherever you resided, so many pastimes took place; preaching pastimes of compassion and mercy, cutting pastimes of honesty and truth, loving pastimes of kindness and trust, and each and every pastime appeared unlimited in its depth of perfection. We were so unqualified, yet we could witness them all. I don’t know why. It was beyond our deserving. It was simply your kindness and mercy upon us.

Sadly, those days are now gone, and so again we must live in separation from you. But we still have your memories, we still have your instructions, and we still have your books. Yes, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for those who remember, you are always present. You hear their prayers, you accept their service, and you bless their hearts. All glories to you!

Your servants at ISKCON New Govardhana, Australia.  
(written by Venugopāla Dāsa)

## New Govardhana (San Diego)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you!

This year marks the fortieth anniversary of your founding ISKCON, and it’s a perfect opportunity to look back and contemplate the wonderful gifts you’ve bestowed on us, and on the whole world. Anyone who is looking for a miracle to inspire faith in God need only look at the history of your ISKCON — how you came to these shores in 1965 with nothing material but everything spiritual: full faith in the holy name and your spiritual master’s order, and the full backing of Lord Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead — and built an international society with thousands of followers and dozens of temples in twelve short years. From the material point of view your mission was hopeless: a lone old man with no money or followers, trying to create an international society based on an esoteric philosophy alien to Western culture. But from the perspective of forty years, we see that your success was assured from the beginning, as much as was Arjuna’s on the Battlefield of Kurukṣetra. “The battle is already won,” says Kṛṣṇa in the *Gītā*. “Just be My instrument.” Armed with faith in this assurance of Kṛṣṇa’s and in the orders of your own spiritual master, you entered the battlefield of New York City, the heart the modern Kali-yuga civilization, and in a few short years came out gloriously successful.

Now, as we look locally at our *yātrā* and farther afield at the ever-expanding international society that is your legacy, we gain a deep appreciation of your efforts and your gifts: the holy name, your instructions in your books and other media, the temples with their all-attractive Deities, festivals of all kinds, *prasādam* for body and soul, the association of *sādhus* from around the world, the holy *dhāmas* both in India and closer to home. The list goes on. You’ve created a whole world of Kṛṣṇa consciousness for us to live in, if we so choose.

Here in San Diego, with the help of our enthusiastic and generous congregation, we are building a permanent home for Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Giridhārī, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, and Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā. The project includes a large new temple, an ashram/guesthouse, and a cultural hall. We pray that this new center will be a beacon of Kṛṣṇa consciousness for all of Southern California, and that it will be a place of pilgrimage for your followers from around the world. Please give us the strength to remain pure by strictly following the principles you have laid down. We know that everything we do simply depends on your mercy.

Your servants at New Govardhana Dhāma, San Diego, California. USA.

## New Govardhana Hill (Puerto Rico)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you on this special day of your Vyāsa-pūjā!

On this most auspicious day we pray to you to shower your mercy on all of us here in Puerto Rico.

In the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Madhya-līlā* 19.132, purport) you write:

In Vṛndāvana there are prākṛta-sahajiyās who say that writing books or even touching books is taboo. . . . According to Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, distributing literature is like playing on a great *mṛdaṅga*. Consequently we always request members of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness to publish as many books as possible and distribute them widely throughout the world.

We are trying to follow this order even though we are so few here in Puerto Rico. You said, “If you want to know me, read my books.” Your books are the basis for spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We are inspired to study your books and teach them to others. In the *Bhagavad-gītā* (18.68) it is said, “For one who explains this supreme secret to the devotees, pure devotional service is guaranteed, and at the end he will come back to Me.” Please let us take this instruction to heart.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us New Govardhana Hill and Their most beautiful Lordships Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, Śrī Girigovardhana, and Śrī Śrī Prahlāda-Nṛsiṁha. From the beginning of childhood you had the opportunity to worship the Deities of Rādhā-Govinda in a playful manner with your sister. The same playful Deities became your most worshipful Deities in proper regulation.

What can we really offer you? Many centers are doing big, big programs and holding wonderful festivals. We are just struggling to maintain the standard of the Deity worship and keep the temple from being taken by the bank. We can never repay our debt to you, but we can only express our love.

*catur-vidha-śrī-bhagavat-prasāda-*  
*svādv-anna-tṛptān hari-bhakta-saṅghān*  
*kṛtvaiva tṛptiṁ bhajataḥ sadaiva*  
*vande guroḥśrī-caraṇāravindam*

“The spiritual master encourages distribution of *prasādam* to the public. Ours is not a dry philosophy—simply talk and go away. No. We distribute *prasādam,* very sumptuous *prasādam*.” (*The Journey of Self-Discovery*) This coming year we will try to improve the quality of *prasādam*. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for *prasādam* and the Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra, which together form the only way to control the tongue. The magic you do is to make everyone chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, even people of other religions. On this day we pray to you for the ability to distribute this Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra to everyone.

Your disciple and granddisciples in ISKCON Puerto Rico.  
(written by Navanīta Taskara Devī Dāsī)

## New Hastināpura

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. We, your devotees in Potomac, Maryland, at the temple of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madana-mohana, give this offering for your Vyāsa-pūjā, when ISKCON is celebrating its fortieth anniversary.

We are very grateful to you for establishing the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement and this uniquely located temple in the Washington, D.C., area. Over the past thirty years, this suburban temple has greatly blessed our family and many friends in many ways. It has given us the opportunity to revive and cultivate our dormant love for Kṛṣṇa with the help of your many able disciples. Without your mercy and their guidance, we would have been thrown into the deep well of nescience. We are still very much struggling with *māyā,* but, thanks to you, now we at least have hope.

We are so fortunate to have had the association of advanced devotees and *sannyāsīs* in the past three decades, especially Satsvarūpa Dāsa Goswami, Bhakti-tīrtha Swami, Gopāl Krishna Goswami, Mukunda Goswami, Romapāda Swami, Navayogendra Swami, Bhakti Svarūpa Dāmodara Swami, Rādhā-Govinda Swami, and many other senior devotees, who have kept us engaged at your lotus feet to serve Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madana-mohana.

Under the leadership of His Grace Anuttama Dāsa, we are trying to realize your dream of making this place beautiful and attractive so that the senators, congressmen, and other officials will visit and take advantage of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We pray to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to please inspire us so that we may fulfill your desires. Please continue to guide us through your many disciples.

Sincerely yours, at your lotus feet,  
Your devotees in New Hastināpura dhāma, Potomac, Maryland, USA.  
(written by Śyāma Kṛṣṇa Dāsa)

## New Jagannātha Purī (Zurich)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda

Another Vyāsa-pūjā, and another opportunity to express our eternal gratitude and loyalty to you, our selfless savior. The magnitude of your great work, like a tidal wave, is yet to be understood. A sixty-nine-year-old *sādhu* sails to America alone — and twelve years later he’s changed the course of history. You elevated us to the position of human beings by giving us the four rules, *prasādam,* and the chanting of the holy names. You gave us devotional service and an opportunity to have a relationship with the lover of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī. Since the *Vedas* sing of your character and your glories are spread all over the three worlds, how can *jīvas* as insignificant as us even dare to praise your greatness?

We pledge our best, although, O beloved master, our best is often flawed and wanting. Often you stressed the temporary nature of all things, save for Kṛṣṇa and His devotional service. Yet how amazing that we, feeble humans, fearlessly embrace this *māyā*, forgetting that it is His divine energy; once we toy with it, we are helplessly pulled by that forceful current of illusion. However we struggle to be *vyavasāyātmikā buddhiḥ,* our devious masters, the traitorous senses, with the complicity of the faithless intelligence, knock us off track. Our days are numbered; still we fail to seriously understand how fallible we are.

Dear master, even then, you always lovingly protect us. Your books talk. They nurture. As time goes by, their importance intensifies. Truth is always truth — past, present, and future — regardless of the current ideology. Our only shelter is faith in the guru’s timeless words.

On this most blessed day of your appearance, let the light of understanding illumine our ignorant hearts and kindle the fire of causeless devotion so that it burns to ashes our tenacious impurities, and let love for Kṛṣṇa, radiating from such pure hearts as yours, attract the living entities to Him. Please bless us, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Your mercy is all that we are made of.

Your insignificant, humble servants,  
The devotees at the New Jagannātha Purī yātrā, in Zurich, Switzerland.

## New Nabdwīp

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace on this, the 110thanniversary of your appearance in this world.  
Sometimes we forget how great you are. Every day we bow to you and chant . . .

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

These *praṇāma-mantras* have become as familiar to us as our very breath, and, like our breath, we can easily take them for granted. That means taking you for granted as well, Śrīla Prabhupāda. When this happens, the Lord has to shake us awake.

And so it happened this year when a World Religions lecturer called to say he was making a DVD movie about “Sacred Sites of Hawaii,” and would we please let him come to interview us and shoot some footage of the temple and grounds. One is always cautious about the motives of such people, but the man sounded sincere, said he used to visit our temple as a student back in the 1970s, and wanted us to present our message “as it is.” So pressing our palms and drawing a deep breath, we consented and fixed a date for the shooting.

Before the date, I approached one of our senior Hawaiian devotees, your KuśāMātājī, to be the main presenter for the interview. Kuśā joined your Honolulu temple as a teenager in 1969 and has consistently proven herself over the years as an exceptionally bright and surrendered servant of Your Divine Grace. She immediately saw the movie’s potential for spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness far and wide and began preparing a script for the interview, with handouts for the lecturer to reference later on.

It was a Sunday morning when Dr. Achilles S.C. Gacis showed up with his two-man crew. The sky was overcast but calm, the palms swaying gently above the fruit and flower trees and freshly-mown grass. When our guests emerged from their vehicle, they went to the trunk to start unloading their equipment but were immediately struck by the beauty and serenity of the temple property. The night-blooming jasmine, the gardenia stars, the mango, pomelo, and papaya trees—all caught their eyes, resting finally on the “holy basil garden” in front of the temple, the sacred abode of Śrīmatī Tulasī Devī.

Big, burly, and bearded, Achilles shook our hands heartily. He was grateful we had let him come film and genuinely touched by the spiritual atmosphere of the temple. He hadn’t been here for many years and had forgotten, as we sometimes do, what transcendental power your temples have. No heel this Achilles. He sounded like a kid come home.

After Kuśā, Śānti-parāyaṇa, and I signed some legal papers about the movie-making, we ushered Achilles and crew up to your quarters to do the interview. As we entered, I bowed to your *mūrti* and began identifying some items in your room. Turning to our guests, I was amazed to see Achilles and his men standing just inside the doorway, still as candles, staring intently past your low desk at your *mūrti*. The place where you had sat, Śrīla Prabhupāda, where you had chanted, counseled, enlightened, admonished, and shared Kṛṣṇa with so many people, was now filled with your *mūrti* dictating your Bhaktivedanta purports, a service you did a lot in Hawaii during your manifest pastimes. Whatever I was saying didn’t seem to matter as our guests stood at attention before you for several minutes. You were present, they knew it, and I woke up.

The interview went well. Sitting beside your desk, Kuśā was brilliant presenting Kṛṣṇa consciousness and glorifying you, who have given us everything. Sometimes the camera focused on Govinda Dāsīs colorful painting of the Pañca-tattva behind you, and sometimes on all your books filling the large, antique bookcase with the sign atop that reads:

Founder-Ācārya of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness His Divine Grace

**A.C. BHAKTIVEDANTA SWAMI PRABHUPĀDA**  
**MEMORIAL TĪRTHA**

More than once, Achilles had to stop the interview to collect himself, overwhelmed by hearing about your and the Lord’s glories. At one point he sent his sound man downstairs to record the melodious chanting coming from the temple room. A mother and daughter visiting from Eastern Europe were singing Hare Kṛṣṇa very beautifully with a harmonium. Achilles told us later that chanting would accompany the still shots his crew took inside and outside the temple. Especially of our giant banyan tree, one of the largest in Hawaii, which stands as a towering specimen of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s metaphor for this entangling material world. (*Gītā* 15.1)

To all of his sacred site hosts, the professor has promised a case of the DVD movie he is making. As soon as it is done, the devotees will show you our segment in your quarters, Śrīla Prabhupāda, the most sacred spot in the temple, as our deities, Śrī Śrī Pañca-tattva, must surely agree.

As for this writer, I’ll be reading you this offering, Lord willing, not from your temple in Honolulu but from your Radhadesh community in Belgium. I am forever indebted to your servants in Hawaii—especially Temple President Tejaprakash Prabhu and his good wife Lakṣmaṇā Mātājīfor allowing me prepare an English degree so that I can teach at your Bhaktivedanta College at Radhadesh. Please continue to bless the devotional endeavors of your devotees in Hawaii. And please keep waking me up so that I may write and teach well in your everlasting loving service. Hare Kṛṣṇa!

Your dear servants in New Nabdwīp, Honolulu, Hawaii, USA.  
(written by Sureśvara Dāsa)

## New Ramaṇ Reti

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet, which traversed this globe to offer a torchlight in this world of darkness.

Standing before you and making a report on local activities was something you liked your disciples to do. With great humility we present our report to you.

*‘sādhu-saṅga’, ‘sādhu-saṅga’—sarva-śāstre kaya*  
*lava-mātra sādhu-saṅge sarva-siddhi haya*

All the *śāstras* recommend the association of devotees. For by such association one will attain all perfection.

So many wonderful devotees have come to reside here in New Ramaṇ Reti, Alachua, Florida. You personally visited this area once and described it as “a most remote corner of the world, far away from Śrī Māyāpur.” In our confused state of life we think our residence and place of birth to be the center of the universe. You reminded us by this statement that the real center of the world is Śrī Māyāpur. Thus, before most of us came here, even before many of us were born, you instructed us not to be misled by pride in one’s place of birth or residence.

Yet this verse from *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* reminds us that the association of devotees is most important. To that end one may become satisfied living here, “in this remote corner of the world.” Here we have three full generations of your followers living and executing Kṛṣṇa consciousness. There are currently over 350 families practicing the principles you have given us in your divine Bhaktivedanta purports and your life of example. We are regularly maintaining a strict daily temple program, *maṅgala-ārati,* Tulasī-pūjā, *japa,* greeting of the Deities, *guru-pūjā,* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* class. And because it is up to the standard as instructed by you, participation is increasing with great enthusiasm. In addition, we have several schools enthusing and encouraging children of all ages to take to this purifying process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. This lays the groundwork for *sādhu-saṅga* in the future.

You often stated that example is better than precept. Here we have many senior *gṛhastha* disciples following your instructions — husbands and wives who have faithfully been executing your instructions together for twenty, twenty-five years and more. Raising children on your instructions, being responsible members of the Vaiṣṇava community. Staying engaged in your service in spite of the obstacles that family life can bring. These senior followers of yours are thus not ordinary householders but are also those *sādhus* whose association is recommended.

Your second generation here has taken strong steps to fulfill your mission as well. Spearheading the need to train the next generation after them, they have developed a wonderful mentoring program. These young men and women take a younger devotee child under their wing and guide them, befriend them, and help lead them down the path toward Kṛṣṇa. These relationships solidify the foundation of devotional service that you envisioned for generations to come. Neither of these two successive generations had your personal association, but by associating with your instructions, your books, and your disciples, they have developed the desire to push forward this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. They have thus assured future generations of *sādhus* to associate with.

The central focus of this community is Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, and Śrī Lakṣmī-Nṛsiṁha. We have over fifty *brāhmaṇas* who carefully follow your instructions for developing a wonderful Deity program here. These men and women have faithfully adhered to your principles of brahminical cleanliness and purity. As a result, the Deity worship here is most beautiful, and we are confident it would please Your Divine Grace very much. As a result, many *sādhus* come to visit here and feel the result of such service.

How are all these programs going on? Have all these *brāhmaṇa* families been imported from India to carry out your mission? No, indeed! These are ordinary boys and girls, men and women, save for one thing—your sublime mercy! While we may appear to be ordinary citizens, we are actually your Vaikuṇṭha people. Being inspired by your service, your determination, your love for guru and Kṛṣṇa, we have imbibed the same from you. These devotees have taken your mercy, your instructions, and your blessings and worked hard to make a community that is a testament to your greatness. A proclamation of your desire to see the whole world become Kṛṣṇa conscious. A confirmation of the power of *sādhu-saṅga*. This is the real beauty of our community.

On this, your Vyāsa-pūjā day, we offer this service, this attitude, this entire community as a gift at your lotus feet and beg most humbly for your mercy to continue to serve you, generation after generation.

Your humble servants at New Ramaṇ Reti Dhāma, Alachua, Florida, USA.

## Auckland (New Varsana)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances prostrated in the dust of your lotus feet.

Living in Vṛndāvana, temple of Rādhā-Dāmodara,  
A resident, yet not there to stay,  
For you have a mission from your master  
That will take you far and away.  
Daily *japa* you chanted with rapt attention,  
Tasting the holy name your only solace,  
Sweet *kathā* and *bhajans* fill the air  
While you have *darśan* of the Lord’s beautiful face.  
As you gaze at Rūpa Gosvāmī’s *samādhi*  
While taking your daily meals,  
Inspiration for your great work  
You constantly feel.  
While Delhi is your work,  
Vṛndāvana is your home,  
Writing, printing, and distributing  
Without a *maṭha* of your own.  
Struggling alone and so it seems,  
Yet undeterred you soldier on,  
The *saṅkīrtan* movement will soon revive  
And will see a new rising dawn.  
Kṛṣṇa has His plan,  
With you as His *senapati.*  
Non-Āryans will soon be  
Performing *kīrtan* and *ārati.*  
Over the ocean you bravely crossed,  
Through seasickness and heart attack,  
To the land of voidism and impersonalism,  
A place where people have gone mad.  
Yet you attracted the boys and girls  
To your simplicity and purity.  
And the chanting of the holy names  
In every town and village becomes a reality.  
Vṛndāvan epitomizes service with love.  
That mood you carried wherever you go,  
Implanting it in foreign soil  
Through your preaching, forceful and bold.  
Each of your temples is a holy *dhāma,*  
Where the Deity resides.  
*Harikīrtan* and *bhāgavata-kathā*  
Your devotees recite.  
What you have done is unprecedented,  
Leaving behind a legacy  
Of spiritual lawbooks and practices  
Allowing one to taste devotional ecstasies.  
Around the world you traveled,  
Planting the flag of Kṛṣṇa everywhere.  
Those who take shelter of you  
Feel your compassion and loving care.  
Your movement is still going strong  
Despite some hiccups now and then.  
What you have established is here to stay;  
The foundation you built with your mighty pen.  
Prabhupāda, you are still with us.  
Your association is there to take  
Through your books and instructions.  
In your mercy we can daily bathe.  
Your legacy is in our hands,  
A burden of love we willingly carry,  
To honor you with our love  
By working in cooperation daily.  
Feeling inadequate and unqualified,  
We submit this humble prayer:  
Please take our hands and light our paths;  
We want be true Prabhupāda followers.

Your eternal servants at New Varṣāṇā, Auckland, New Zealand.  
(written by Cirañjīva Dāsa)

## New Vraja-dhāma (Hungary)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances! All glories to you and the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu!

In the historical past Mahārāja Pṛthu appeared in a very tense situation with the aim to show the world the right path. Perceiving his greatness, the sages started to glorify him with the following prayer:

*athāpy udāra-śravasaḥ pṛthor hareḥ*  
*kalāvatārasya kathāmṛtādṛtāḥ*  
*yathopadeśaṁ munibhiḥ pracoditāḥ*  
*ślāghyāni karmāṇi vayaṁ vitanmahi*

**“Although we are unable to glorify you adequately, we nonetheless have a transcendental taste for glorifying your activities. We shall try to glorify you according to the instructions received from authoritative sages and scholars. Whatever we speak, however, is always inadequate and very insignificant. Dear King, because you are a direct incarnation of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, all your activities are liberal and ever laudable.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **4.16.3.)**

Mankind is in a very difficult situation again, but your appearance has given us new hope to turn to the right path.

I humbly pray to my spiritual master, His Holiness Śivarāma Swami Mahārāja, that by his mercy I am able to serve you with my words as well on this glorious day, the anniversary of the day the world got your exceptional association.

We recently visited the holy land of Bhārata with fifty devotees. Some of us have been in the movement a long time, spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Hungary. We commended ourselves to the shelter of Śrī Māyāpur *dhāma,* Śrī Jagannātha Purī*dhāma,* and Śrī Vṛndāvana *dhāma*. We were seeking the mercy of the holy places and the Vaiṣṇavas so that we would be able to accomplish our service in New Vraja-dhāma and deepen our faith in and love for Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara and Their devotees.

It was a great pleasure to see how nicely everything you established in these holy places (and also outside them) is being maintained, and at what a rapid pace everything is being developed by the devotees. Although the influence of the Age of Kali is very apparent, ISKCON radiates strength all over the world.

*aparādha kṣamāila, ḍubila prema-jale*  
*kebā eḍāibe prabhura prema-mahājāle*

**“Lord Caitanya excused them all, and they merged into the ocean of devotional service, for no one can escape the unique loving network of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi-līlā*** **7.37)**

ŚrīGaurāṅga Mahāprabhu is weaving His net, and He sends persons who are the most efficient and who distribute His unlimited mercy the greatest. He intended for you the glory of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the world, and because of your pure devotion it was accomplished. Though we look at all this from the distance of only one generation, still, your transcendental activities are inconceivable.

Lots of things happened in a short time. You sowed the seeds everywhere and left it to your exceptional disciples to tend the delicate creepers of devotion so that they would bear fruit, and so that new seeds would be sowed in new places.

“Human reason fails to understand how by serving the devotee *Bhāgavata* or the book *Bhāgavata* one gets gradual promotion on the path of devotion.” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.2.18, purport)

“Pure devotion is as much infectious, in a good sense, as infectious diseases. A pure devotee is cleared from all kinds of sins.” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.5.25, purport)

“There are many things which hinder one from entering devotional service. But by the association of pure devotees all these obstacles are removed. . . . Infection of the qualities of the pure devotee means to imbibe the taste of pure devotion always in the transcendental activities of the Personality of Godhead.” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.5.25, purport)

Now New Vraja-dhāma has entered the thirteenth year of its existence. Over these years, because of the considerable efforts of His Holiness Śivarāma Swami, a firm Vaiṣṇava community has been formed, an adequate background has been developed here, and, above all, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara have come to inspire us and mercifully accept the devotion of the devotees.

You said that farm communities would give devotees a chance for a Kṛṣṇa conscious life, and for showing an example to of *varṇāśrama* society. Although in many respects we have not been able to fulfill your desires yet, we are trying to comply with your request to live plainly and preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness to everyone. We are very grateful to you for your directions. Every day we come face to face with the great treasure of the principles you laid down for the upliftment of this distorted world.

We would like to ask for your blessings so that we are able to live a simple life based on agriculture and cow protection, a life that would be an example for others. In this way, new Kṛṣṇa conscious farm communities may be established either in Hungary or elsewhere in the world.

We are striving to increase our self-sufficiency, striking out on our own in many fields — producing our own food and clothes, building houses, etc. At the same time, we are arranging to have more and more visitors come so that preaching can develop as well, hopefully evoking the mercy of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, on behalf of our community I offer all our activities for the service of your lotus feet, and I request your mercy so that we can fulfill your transcendental plans, both now and in the future as well.

Please help our community practice proper *sādhu-saṅga* and *sādhu-sevā,* and help us ward off conflicts and offenses among the devotees.

Your servants in New Vraja-dhāma, Hungary.  
(written by Pārtha Dāsa)

## New York (Brooklyn)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**

**Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Again we rejoice in the nectar for which we always hanker. With joy and a deep sense of gratitude we relish the opportunity to offer homage to your divine grace and sing songs of appreciation for you, befitting your special position as the founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON, the preeminent *śikṣā-guru* for all those disciples fortunate enough, and sincere enough, to realize that beneficent connection with Your Divine Grace. Today we recommit our hearts at your lotus feet on this most blessed occasion of your wonderful Vyāsa-pūjā and glorious appearance day. Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindajī certainly smile blissfully on this blessed event.

**Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa-Dhyāna**

*sat-puṇḍarīka-nayanaṁ meghāvbhaṁ vaidyutāmbaram*  
*dvi-bhujaṁ jñāna-mudrāḍhyaṁ vana-mālinam īśvaram*

*divyālaṅkāraṇopetaṁ sakhībhiḥ pariveṣṭitam*  
*cid-ānanda-ghanaṁ kṛṣṇaṁ rādhāliṅgita-vigraham*

*śrī-kṛṣṇaṁśrī-ghana-śyāmaṁ pūrṇānanda-kalevaram*  
*dvi-bhujaṁ sarva-deveśaṁ rādhāliṅgita-vigraham*

I meditate on the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, who possesses beautiful lotus eyes, whose color is that of a new cloud, whose clothes are like lightning, who possesses two arms and a beautiful forest garland, and whose hand is indicating divine knowledge by exhibiting the *jñāna-mudrā*. That Kṛṣṇa is decorated with glittering ornaments and is surrounded by all the cowherd girlfriends of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī. While being embraced by Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī Herself, His form is intensified consciousness and bliss. Śrī Kṛṣṇa, who is intensely bluish-black and has two arms, is the Lord of all the demigods, and His body is full of transcendental bliss. \[*Pañcarātra-Pradīpa* 3.5: “Additional Verses for Worshiping Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa”\]

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have benefited our lives with matchless good fortune. We were poor and now we are rich, we were lame and now we dance in ecstasy, we were dumb but now recite eloquent praise of a most praiseworthy lover of Kṛṣṇa, we were deaf and now relish nectar by hearing sweet expressions of love in glorification of Your Divine Grace, we were dead yet again we live, eternally in bliss with knowledge of your beloved and dearest friends Śrī Govindajī and Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī. Who among us will not offer all we have, body and mind, home and family, wealth and actions, our heart and our soul to you Śrīla Prabhupāda. If we truly know, understand, and love you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is there anything we would not sacrifice for you to bring a blissful smile to your lotus mouth and please Your Divine Grace? Because of the great sacrifices you made for our benefit, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are now concentrating our minds and hearts on the all-attractive nature of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindajīs glories. We are no longer drowning in the ocean of mundane sorrows. We thank you again and again for picking us up, tolerating our faults, and guiding us with your enlightened vision to the topmost rung of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

*ei-bāro karuṇā koro vaiṣṇava gosāi*  
*patita-pāvana tomā bine keho nāi*

**O Vaiṣṇava Gosvāmī, please be merciful to me now. There is no one except you who can purify the fallen souls. (“Vaiṣṇave Vijñapti,” by Śrīla Narottama Dāsa Ṭhākura)**

***He reasons ill who tells that Vaiṣṇavas die***  
***When thou art living still in sound!***  
**\[Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura\]**

Our mission in pleasing you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is to introduce your association into the lives of the unfortunate, who know not truth and see not light. Your Kṛṣṇa consciousness, expounded in your Bhaktivedanta purports, and your wise counsel, offered in thousands of discourses on Kṛṣṇa consciousness, uncover and reawaken our eternal *divya-jñāna*. By our worship of Your Divine Grace and by our practicing the blissful *sādhana* of *kṛṣṇa-bhakti,* our hearts become cleansed of the tamasic dust accumulated for eons. Every aspiring devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa has the great benediction of being able to read your potent writings, to physically see and hear your inspiring recorded discourses. Yes, we have this golden opportunity, unavailable to any other religionist or aspiring transcendentalist, to hear directly from such a great, pure devotee as you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. To hear the Kṛṣṇa conscious sound vibration *as it is*, as you speak and purely chant it. We are blessed to hear from your lotus mouth the pure chanting of the holy names of Kṛṣṇa, the omnipotent Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*. Would any sincere, dedicated, and intelligent student of spirituality neglect to gain spiritual benediction from such potent contact, with such a great saintly and empowered representative of Lord Kṛṣṇa? Please teach us how to follow the process of pure *kṛṣṇa-bhakti* perfectly, and please empower us to lead others to the blissful platform of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

*jāhāra nikaṭe gele pāpa dūre jāy*  
*emona doyāla prabhu kebā kothā pāy*

**Where can anyone find such a merciful personality, whose mere audience sends all sins far away? \[“Vaiṣṇave Vijñapti,” by Śrīla Narottama Dāsa Ṭhākura\]**

When we were fortunate to have your *vapu* association, the potency of your pure *kṛṣṇa-bhakti*, your *kṛṣṇa-prema*, affected our hearts and impelled us to act in spiritually correct and seemingly ecstatic manners. While *śāstra* wisely informs us that *vāṇī* is more important than *vapu*, still those potent, ecstatic, and empowering encounters, however brief, with Your Divine Grace, the pure *kṛṣṇa-bhakta* of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindajī, the pure *kṛṣṇa-bhakta* of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nityānanda, provided us with a flood of miraculous mercy that must not be underestimated. Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, the great preacher of the principles of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, has stressed the importance of association with *sādhus*, pure devotees of the Lord. He said that even a moment’s association with a pure devotee can enable one to achieve all perfection.

Therefore, remembrances of your awesome and potent *vapu* association reveal the Kṛṣṇa conscious path and lead aspiring pure *kṛṣṇa-bhaktas* forward, in their progressive march toward Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Such remembrances inspire the devotees to make the Śrīla Prabhupāda devotional connection, in every possible way, and to grab hold of the vision and higher taste of your transcendental, ecstatic *vāṇī* association.

*gaṅgāra paraśa hoile paścate pāvan*  
*darśane pavitra koro-ei tomāra guṇ*

**One becomes purified after bathing in the waters of the sacred Ganges many times, but just the sight of you purifies the fallen souls. This is your great power. \[“Vaiṣṇave Vijñapti,” by Śrīla Narottama Dāsa Ṭhākura)**

In the words of *śāstra*, and in your own words of truth, we see perfectly the greatness, potency, and purifying effect of your transcendental state of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Anyone who has known you even for brief moments of mercy can understand your consciousness to be on a different wavelength, even an other-worldly wavelength — the spiritual-world wavelengths of *kṛṣṇa-loka*, *prema-bhakti* vibrations. The philosopher’s-stone effect you have on souls fortunate enough to spiritually encounter Your Divine Grace saves them from the clutches of *māyā* and plants the potent and healthy seed of Gaura-Nitāi and Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa consciousness in their blessed hearts. When iron touches a philosopher’s stone, the iron turns to gold. Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura said that the position of a Vaiṣṇava can be tested by seeing how good a philosopher’s stone he is, i.e., by seeing how many Vaiṣṇavas he has made during his life. A Vaiṣṇava should be a philosopher’s stone so that he can raise others to the level of Vaiṣṇava by his preaching, even though people may be fallen and sinful. *Kaniṣṭha-adhikārī* devotees cannot turn others into Vaiṣṇavas, but a *madhyama-adhikārī* Vaiṣṇava can do so by preaching. Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu advised His followers to increase the number of Vaiṣṇavas. You, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, are a wonderful Kṛṣṇa conscious philosopher’s stone, the likes of which we have never seen before. The *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Madhya-līlā* 17.48–49) nicely delineates this:

Translation: When someone heard the chanting of the holy name from the mouth of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and someone else heard this chanting from that second person, and someone again heard this chanting from the third person, everyone in all countries became a Vaiṣṇava through such disciplic succession. Thus everyone chanted the holy name of Kṛṣṇa and Hari, and they danced, cried, and smiled.

Purport: The transcendental potency of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* is herein explained. First, the holy name is vibrated by Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. When someone hears from Him directly, he is purified. When another person hears from that person, he also is purified. In this way the purification process is advanced among pure devotees. Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu is the Supreme Personality of Godhead, and no one can claim His potency. Nonetheless, if one is a pure devotee, hundreds and thousands of men can be purified by his vibration. This potency is within every living being, provided he chants the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* offenselessly and without material motives. When a pure devotee chants offenselessly, another person will become a Vaiṣṇava, and from him another Vaiṣṇava will emerge. This is the *paramparā* system.

*\* \* \**

And from Narottama Dāsa Ṭhākura we learn,

*hari-sthāne aparādhe tāre hari-nām*  
*tomā sthāne aparādhe nāhi paritrāṇ*

**The holy name delivers one who has offended Lord Hari, but deliverance is impossible if one offends you. \[“Vaiṣṇave Vijñapti”\]**

Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, while instructing Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī, has compared the devotional service of the Lord to a creeper. A creeper has a feeble stem and requires the support of a tree to grow, and while growing, it requires sufficient protection so that it may not be lost. While describing the system of protection for the creeper of devotional service, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu has especially stressed protection from offenses unto the lotus feet of Vaiṣṇavas. Such offenses are called *vaiṣṇava-aparādha*. If one commits *vaiṣṇava-aparādha*, all of his progress in devotional service will be checked. Even though one is very much advanced in devotional service, if he commits offenses at the feet of a Vaiṣṇava, his advancement is all spoiled. The most grievous type of *vaiṣṇava-aparādha* is called *guru-aparādha*, which refers to offenses at the lotus feet of the spiritual master. In the chanting of the holy name of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, this *guru-aparādha* is considered the most grievous offense. Please bless us with your mercy and love, Śrīla Prabhupāda, so we may always strive to be sincere, so we may always strive to have a humble service attitude, and so we may always strive to learn more about how to love Kṛṣṇa as Your Divine Grace guides us perfectly on the path back home, Back to Godhead.

*tomāra hṛdoye sadā govinda-viśrām*  
*govinda kohena-mora vaiṣṇava parāṇ*

**Your heart is always the resting place of Lord Govinda, and Lord Govinda says, “The Vaiṣṇavas are in My heart.” \[“Vaiṣṇave Vijñapti,” by Śrīla Narottama Dāsa Ṭhākura\]**

The devotees, out of their extreme love for Govinda, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, always carry the Lord within their hearts. The Lord is already in the heart of everyone, but the Vaiṣṇavas and the *brāhmaṇas* actually always see Him in their ecstasy. Therefore *brāhmaṇas* and Vaiṣṇavas are carriers of Lord Viṣṇu. When the *brāhmaṇas* and Vaiṣṇavas are pleased with a person, Lord Viṣṇu is also pleased. This is confirmed by Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura in his eight verses praising the spiritual master: *yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*. By pleasing the spiritual master, who is both a *brāhmaṇa* and a Vaiṣṇava, one pleases the Supreme Personality of Godhead. If the Supreme Personality of Godhead is pleased, then one has nothing more to achieve, either in this world or after death. Our goal, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is to sacrifice all we have, our entire lives, at your lotus feet. We feel that nothing short of this sacrifice will suffice in our endeavor to please you and to try to repay you for the greatest mercy, the greatest care, and the greatest true love we shall ever receive.

*prati-janme kori āśā caraṇera dhūli*  
*narottame koro doyāāpanāra boli’*

**I desire to have the dust of your holy feet in every birth I may take. Please consider Narottama yours, and be kind upon him. \[“Vaiṣṇave Vijñapti,” by Śrīla Narottama Dāsa Ṭhākura\]**

A sincere devotee is wise to take shelter of a pure devotee such as Your Divine Grace. That is the real solution to the problems on the devotional path. One must approach a pure devotee, either through *vāṇī* or *vapu* association, a great personality who is *niṣkiñcana,* uninterested in material life, and take the purifying, empowering dust of such a pure devotee’s lotus feet on his head. As long as one does not approach such a great personality, there is no possibility of becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious. As soon as we approach the pure devotee and place the dust of his lotus feet on our heads, we are immediately freed from all this material nonsense (*anartha-apagamaḥ*). That means we will forget the foolish idea that material activities and goals are important in life. This is the Kṛṣṇa consciousness process. So we have to approach one who is *niṣkiñcana*, who has nothing to do with this material world, and such a great personality is you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Your only ambition, your only goal, is to increase the service to the all-attractive Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, to teach us how to love Kṛṣṇa.

So once again we take great delight in offering our heartfelt homage, our humble offering of deep gratitude and appreciation for all you have given us, for all that Kṛṣṇa consciousness reveals to us, now and into eternity. We beg forgiveness for our offenses, and we beg for eternal devotional service at your lotus feet.

Begging to remain your menial servants forever,  
Your insignificant children at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindajī Mandira, Brooklyn, New York, USA.  
(written by Rāmabhadra Dāsa)

## New York (26 Second Avenue)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We offer you our prostrated obeisances again and again in this holiest of holy places. Meditating on your pastimes here at 26 Second Avenue has been just one of the matchless gifts I’ve received since coming here.

**Prabhupāda in the Big Apple**

While vibes of chanting lingered in the air  
And hippies heads still spun from Swami’s prayer,  
He picked an apple from a wooden bowl  
And peeled and cut it deftly as the whole  
Group watched. Then with aristocratic grace  
He popped one piece — not touching hand to face —  
Into his mouth. And passed around the rest  
Of his *prasād,* a piece for every guest.  
Then casually he turned his head and spat  
The seeds off the wall, to the floor, near the dais where he sat.  
Now tell me why the ones who watched were awed?  
Can simple feats like this give love for God?  
Just see the strength he had to catch our fall.  
And so he dealt with apples big and small.

It has been said that “The quality of mercy is not strained. It droppeth as a gentle rain from heaven.” The fact that so many people were struck by the way you shared a piece of fruit with them shows how effortlessly you showered mercy on everyone. If such an insignificant event could melt people’s hearts, then what must it have been like to hear you chant the holy name, or speak on the *Bhagavad-gītā*?

But the recordings you made of your lectures here at 26 Second Avenue reveal a deeper truth unseen by Shakespeare when he penned the above line. Anyone who listens can hear how you did in fact strain yourself to deliver the message of *kṛṣṇa-bhakti* to people steeped in . . . well, New York. There was a harsh edge to your voice in those classes. Can I say it almost sounds like desperation? How intensely you must have cried to Kṛṣṇa for the salvation of the lost souls here. But then you weren’t interested in gentle rains, were you? You had torrential downpours in mind. What else can cleanse such a filthy place?

And how the cloud bank of mercy drenched this city! Hundreds of devotees flooded the streets with *prasādam*, books, and the holy name. But alas, time has been cruel to us since your departure. The King and Queen of New York have left Manhattan and now watch affairs from the other side of the river, served with the greatest attention and love by a handful of your loyal servants. On the island itself a small band of us, mostly your grandchildren, serve you here at your original home.

Although we try to carry on your mission, it seems that things are different now. When we gather in the streets and beat our drums, the people hardly seem to notice. When we visit Tompkins Square Park, no one gathers around like they did when you were here. When we pass out your books the people take them, yes, but how well they are able to understand them is apparent by the distance they keep from your movement. Of course we feel purified when we do such things, but in our hearts we long for so much more to happen here.

So we look to the heavens and we wonder, “Will those sweet rains come again?”Śrīla Prabhupāda, sometimes it seems like we’re drying up and dying in this drought. Will you cry for us again? If just one of your oceanic tears fell on this city, it would cause another inundation.

Your servants at 26 Second Avenue, New York, USA.  
(written by Rādhā-vallabha Dāsa)

## New Delhi (Noida)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet! All glories to Vyāsa-pūjā day!

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda, this is the day all your children and grandchildren wait for the whole year. We never knew the meaning of Vyāsa-pūjā until you bestowed your causeless mercy upon us. You gave us the most precious gift, the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra,* which was chanted by Lord Caitanya, Haridāsa Ṭhākura, the Six Gosvāmīs, and the other Vaiṣṇavas in this divine *paramparā*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the most magnanimous distributor of the holy name of the Lord. In a morning lecture in Honolulu on May 29, 1976, you said:

“Kṛṣṇa” means all-attractive. This name can attract everyone. This is the most perfect. Out of many names, the Kṛṣṇa name is *mukhya,* or the primary. So Caitanya Mahāprabhu has introduced Kṛṣṇa name, *kṛṣṇa-varṇam*. . . . It is Lord Caitanya’s special benediction that we can chant, even without proper understanding, and still become ecstatic in love of Kṛṣṇa. If we take advantage of the mercy of Lord Caitanya, then the results of our chanting will be wonderful, as they were for Ajāmila. . . . So take this opportunity of chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa. . . . . Your life is saved; your next life is saved.

As we attempt to write something that describes the significance of your divine appearance in our life and in the lives of so many others, we realize that we have very little realization or even qualification to describe such a thing. What we feel we *do* know, though, is that the great sacrifices you made for the welfare and eternal benefit of others deserve much more glorification than what we can humbly try to offer today. How rare such a pure and selfless soul is in this world! How rare indeed. There are many who say they know how to love, while they simply live a life of lust. But you did not just speak of love; you knew what love was and you distributed the highest love at the highest level.

*mac-cittā mad-gata-prāṇā bodhayantaḥ parasparam*  
*kathayantaś ca māṁ nityaṁ tuṣyanti ca ramanti ca*

**“The thoughts of My pure devotees dwell in Me, their lives are fully devoted to My service, and they derive great satisfaction and bliss from always enlightening one another and conversing about Me.” (*****Bhagavad-gītā*** **10.9)**

Pure devotees, whose characteristics are mentioned here, engage themselves fully in the transcendental loving service of the Lord. Their minds cannot be diverted from the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa. Their talks are solely on the transcendental subjects. We see all these transcendental characteristics in you, Śrīla Prabhupāda

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we would like to take this opportunity to inform you that the construction of your new project here — the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda Mandira and Bhaktivedanta Academy for Spiritual Science — is progressing steadily under the guidance of your beloved disciples His Holiness Gopāl Krishna Goswami and His Holiness Lokanāth Swami. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are trying to develop a very big and unique project here in Noida, but we feel we are not pure and expert enough to raise adequate funds at the right pace. That’s why the construction is taking so long. And with the price of building materials going up, the cost of the project is increasing. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please be merciful upon us and guide us through this time of trial, as you did when you were with us physically, always expertly guiding your disciples and solving all their problems worldwide.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your pleasure we organized a “Vedic Wisdom Contest” on the auspicious anniversary of the advent of the *Bhagavad-gītā*. Almost all the twenty-two leading schools of Noida sent contestants, and about four hundred students and their families cheered for their schoolmates. Most of them visited this temple for the first time. The atmosphere was simply blissful, and the program was a great success. We are going to make this as an annual event. This was the biggest interschool competition ever organized in Noida. ISKCON was in all the newspapers for the whole week with pictures and stories. Participants, their teachers, and parents were extremely happy to see this big competition conducted in a very organized and a pleasant way. Our congregation was ecstatic at the end of the program. The result is that we now have a big entry into educational institutions for our future preaching programs.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are always with us. On every page of your books you are personally speaking about Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us so that we can overcome our false ego and work together in harmony to push on Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s movement.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

Your indebted servants from ISKCON Noida, New Delhi, India.

## Nouvelle Māyāpur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

As time goes by, we are realizing your perfect vision for rural ISKCON communities, and we are beginning to understand how these communities should develop. You envisioned self-sufficient centers, with devotees harvesting their own food, tending cows, producing milk and ghee, and thus giving practical examples of simple and healthy living. By living in this way, devotees can offer pure and high-quality preparations to the Deities, and all ISKCON members are benefited.

This year we celebrate the thirtieth anniversary of the appearance at Nouvelle Māyāpur of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma. Recollecting the times you personally visited this community, we remember your instructions on how important it is to develop our own means of livelihood in our rural communities. You taught us how ISKCON can earn respect and positive public opinion by doing so.

We are eventually learning and confirming your perfect vision for this movement as we experience Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Despite many obstacles and struggles, we are trying our best to follow your directions. For those who have faith that you are our true and eternal well-wisher, peace, prosperity, and the assurance of returning home, back to Godhead, are guaranteed. All glories to your divine instructions!

Your insignificant servants in Nouvelle Māyāpur, France.

## Pandharpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our most respectful obeisances. All glories to you on your most auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā day!

You are the lion who wrestles with the fools and rascals espousing impersonalism and voidism.

You are saving people around the world from their own mental gymnastics and speculations.

You are the inspiration for a myriad of devotional projects that are the modern-day pastimes of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

One such place, where your devotees have gathered to continue your legacy under the guidance of your dear disciple His Holiness Lokanāth Swami, is in Pandharpur, in the heart of Maharashtra. Pandharpur is the spiritual vortex of Maharashtra. It is a popular spiritual pilgrimage site attracting devotees from across India and the world.

Here at your ISKCON center in Pandharpur, located on the banks of the sacred river Chandrabhaga, devotee preachers have gathered and formed the ISKCON Maharashtra Preaching Ministry (IMPM). The mission statement of the IMPM is as follows:

To systematically and solidly, as well as with love and care, guide and train old and new devotees so that they will become expert, dedicated, and humble servants and thus will be recognized by the Maharashtrian population as ideal models of trustworthy ISKCON leaders/devotees and in that capacity will be an inspiration for them so that they will take up their own spiritual practice seriously.

In addition to being a training base for your preachers all over Maharashtra and the world, ISKCON Pandharpur is striving to become a self-sufficient farm community. We are growing most of the *sabjīs* we need. We also have a *gośālā*. This year on Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura Mahārāja Prabhupāda’s appearance day, one of our cows gave birth to a baby bull. We named the bull Bhaktisiddhānta. This is the first calf born in ISKCON Pandharpur.

Just as the mother cow continually nourishes her calf with milk, please continue to nourish ISKCON Pandharpur with your mercy. Your mercy is all that we are made of.

Your grateful servants at ISKCON Pandharpur, India.  
(written by Ekalavya Dāsa)

## Paris

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We reminiscence about the moments you where physically present in Paris, emphasizing the importance of preaching Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s message to the citizens here since the city is considered one of the most important in the world. You gave us suggestions on how to attract the intelligent and influential members of society, and on how to inspire them to chant the holy names of Kṛṣṇa and thus make their lives happy. Consequently, you said, they would help spread the same message throughout France.

We have just opened a preaching center in Paris, and we are working hard to provide a new home for Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Parisīśvara, the Deities you personally installed. This attempt is a combined effort of Vaiṣṇavas from various countries and *yātrās,* and this inspiring teamwork is increasing the faith of many members and leaders of your family.

You taught us the importance of cooperating with one another, of helping one another in this preaching mission. We are now following your advice in France, and we are experiencing the benefits of unity in every aspect of our lives.

Please give us your blessings and shelter so that our efforts at reviving the French *yātrā* bear fruit. We look forward to giving you good news concerning this endeavor in your next Vyāsa-pūjā book.

Your humble servants at ISKCON Paris, France.

## Perth

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

On this most auspicious day of your divine appearance, we make this offering to you. Our offering to you is just like taking the Gaṅgā water from Mother Gaṅgā and offering it back to her. But as you said, everything belongs to Kṛṣṇa anyway, and it is our devotional attitude (consciousness) that Kṛṣṇa is asking for and accepting. Similarly, the words and the statements you gave to us are all that we have to offer to you, so we pray that you please accept this offering.

*rahūgaṇaitat tapasā na yāti*  
*na cejyayā nirvapaṇād gṛhād vā*  
*na cchandasā naiva jalāgni-sūryair*  
*vinā mahat-pāda-rajo-’bhiṣekam*

**My dear King Rahūgaṇa, unless one has the opportunity to smear his entire body with the dust of the lotus feet of great devotees, one cannot realize the Absolute Truth. One cannot realize the Absolute Truth simply by observing celibacy \[*****brahmacarya*****\], strictly following the rules and regulations of householder life, leaving home as a** ***vānaprastha,*** **accepting** ***sannyāsa,*** **or undergoing severe penances in winter by keeping oneself submerged in water or surrounding oneself in summer by fire and the scorching heat of the sun. There are many other processes to understand the Absolute Truth, but the Absolute Truth is revealed only to one who has attained the mercy of a great devotee. \[*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **5.12.12\]**

So, the devotees of Perth are taking shelter of this mercy (dust), which you have bestowed upon us in the form of your words in your books, especially in your purports. Also, we are so fortunate that we have been awarded with the association of your perfect spiritual sons, who are always keeping us in touch with you.

Your purpose in establishing a center in every town and village is so that all living entities can get a chance to purify themselves and revive their relationship with Kṛṣṇa and go back home, back to Godhead. This is happening by your causeless mercy. We would like to present the following to you as an offering: book distribution, *harināma* every Saturday, and *prasādam* distribution, since you once said that in Australia the best weapon for giving the Lord’s mercy is *prasādam*. Sure enough, we have found that *prasādam* distribution has been successful all over Australia — in colleges, universities, festivals, and seminars, and indeed at any kind of function. And what to speak of our restaurants, and especially Food for Life! Here in Perth we have very strong *gṛhasthas* who serve the center in various ways, including maintaining nice Deity worship and a full temple program. We recently began the Nama-haṭṭa program, which is a strong weapon to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Please bless these children and the grandchildren of yours, who are trying to serve your words and carry on with full enthusiasm. With your blessings we can purify ourselves, purify others, serve the purpose of your mission, and go back home, back to Godhead.

Your humble servants at Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi Mandira in Perth, Australia.

## Philadelphia

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Your appearance is certainly the sweet mercy of Kṛṣṇa. In turn, you have given us Kṛṣṇa. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us that we will take full advantage of your kindness, and please bless us that we will, out of gratitude, help others become Kṛṣṇa conscious.

Your temple and congregational devotees in Philadelphia, Pennsylvania, USA.

## Prahlāda &amp; Kuntī Devī Āśrama

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
All glories to Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi!  
All glories to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madana-Gopāla!

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my prostrated obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. On this special occasion, by the grace of my spiritual master, His Holiness Bhakti Rāghava Swami, I would like to try to glorify you, my *paraṁ-gurudeva,* Śrīla Prabhupāda.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given so much mercy to this world. In particular, through your books and your servant, my spiritual master, you have taught this fallen soul to become Kṛṣṇa conscious. Due to your mercy and compassion, you came to Indonesia to give us Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, even though I have never seen you directly, your servant, my spiritual master, and your books have taught me how to take up the process of devotional service to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Bhakti Rāghava Swami has given me full support and advice on how to practice devotional service in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your mercy, Bhakti Rāghava Swami has established a *varṇāśrama-dharma* village in Indonesia. In the province of Lampung he has established the Prahlāda & Kuntī DevīĀśrama as a student hostel for devotees, and now, by your mercy and Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s mercy, he also has established the Gītā Nāgarī Baru village of Tulang Bawang, Lampung, Indonesia.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, I have studied Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Prahlāda & Kuntī DevīĀśrama, and by your blessings and those of my spiritual master I have come to understand Kṛṣṇa consciousness more deeply. I pray for your blessings always, so that I may increase my spiritual knowledge in devotional service. I and all the devotee students at Prahlāda & Kuntī DevīĀśrama practice devotional service to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa by attending the *āratis* and *kīrtanas* in the morning and evening, by chanting the *mahā-mantra,* by following the four regulative principles, by reading your books, and by attempting to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness as per your desire.

Please give us your mercy so that we may continue our devotional service to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa under all conditions and thus become eternal servants of your servant.

Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Your servants at the Prahlada & Kuntī DevīĀśrama, Bandar Lampung , Lampung, Indonesia.
